### [1] [Channel-adaptive Cross-modal Generative Semantic Communication for Point Cloud Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03211)
*Wanting Yang,Zehui Xiong,Qianqian Yang,Ping Zhang,Merouane Debbah,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于点云传输的信道自适应跨模态生成式语义通信框架GenSeC-PC，利用图像作为侧信息，结合扩散模型解码与模拟传输，实现高效、鲁棒的点云实时通信。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着自动驾驶和扩展现实的快速发展，点云的高效传输日益重要。现有方法存在压缩效率不足、对信道条件敏感、依赖无差错的边信息传输等问题，亟需一种鲁棒且高效的点云传输方案。

Method: 提出GenSeC-PC框架：采用融合图像与点云的跨模态语义编码器（图像作为非传输侧信息）；基于PointDif骨干构建解码器；设计非对称的信道自适应联合语义信道编码架构（仅编码器需反馈平均SNR和可用带宽）；采用修正去噪扩散隐式模型加速解码至毫秒级；支持全模拟传输，无需无差错边信息传输。

Result: 实验验证了跨模态语义提取和双指标引导微调的有效性；框架在低SNR、带宽受限、不同数量的2D图像及未见物体等多样条件下均表现出鲁棒性；相比PointDif，实现了更高的压缩效率和更优的重建性能。

Conclusion: GenSeC-PC通过跨模态生成式语义通信方案，在点云传输中实现了高效压缩、鲁棒重建和实时通信，为未来自动驾驶和扩展现实中的点云通信提供了可行的技术路径。

Original Abstract: With the rapid development of autonomous driving and extended reality, efficient transmission of point clouds (PCs) has become increasingly important. In this context, we propose a novel channel-adaptive cross-modal generative semantic communication (SemCom) for PC transmission, called GenSeC-PC. GenSeC-PC employs a semantic encoder that fuses images and point clouds, where images serve as non-transmitted side information. Meanwhile, the decoder is built upon the backbone of PointDif. Such a cross-modal design not only ensures high compression efficiency but also delivers superior reconstruction performance compared to PointDif. Moreover, to ensure robust transmission and reduce system complexity, we design a streamlined and asymmetric channel-adaptive joint semantic-channel coding architecture, where only the encoder needs the feedback of average signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and available bandwidth. In addition, rectified denoising diffusion implicit models is employed to accelerate the decoding process to the millisecond level, enabling real-time PC communication. Unlike existing methods, GenSeC-PC leverages generative priors to ensure reliable reconstruction even from noisy or incomplete source PCs. More importantly, it supports fully analog transmission, improving compression efficiency by eliminating the need for error-free side information transmission common in prior SemCom approaches. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of cross-modal semantic extraction and dual-metric guided fine-tuning, highlighting the framework's robustness across diverse conditions, including low SNR, bandwidth limitations, varying numbers of 2D images, and previously unseen objects.

中文摘要翻译: 随着自动驾驶和扩展现实的快速发展，点云（PC）的高效传输变得日益重要。在此背景下，我们提出了一种新颖的信道自适应跨模态生成式语义通信（SemCom）框架用于点云传输，称为GenSeC-PC。GenSeC-PC采用了一种融合图像与点云的语义编码器，其中图像作为非传输的边信息。同时，解码器基于PointDif骨干网络构建。这种跨模态设计不仅确保了高压缩效率，而且相比PointDif提供了更优的重建性能。此外，为了确保鲁棒传输并降低系统复杂度，我们设计了一种精简且非对称的信道自适应联合语义信道编码架构，其中仅编码器需要平均信噪比（SNR）和可用带宽的反馈。同时，采用修正去噪扩散隐式模型将解码过程加速至毫秒级，从而实现实时点云通信。与现有方法不同，GenSeC-PC利用生成式先验，即使从含有噪声或不完整的源点云中也能确保可靠重建。更重要的是，它支持全模拟传输，通过消除先前语义通信方法中常见的无差错边信息传输需求，提高了压缩效率。仿真结果证实了跨模态语义提取和双指标引导微调的有效性，突显了该框架在低SNR、带宽受限、不同数量的2D图像以及未见物体等多种条件下的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [2] [Pulmonary Embolism Risk Stratification from CTPA and Medical Records: Vascular Graphs Are Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25956)
*Nathan Painchaud,Tristan Habémont,Morgane des Ligneris,Allan Serva,Pierre Croisille,Laurent Bertoletti,Thomas Lampert,Johannes F. Lutzeyer,Odyssée Merveille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究表明，仅凭医疗记录和CTPA影像中的心脏生物标志物即可进行肺栓塞风险分层，血管生物标志物和血管图神经网络无法提供额外的判别信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 肺栓塞风险分层对临床决策至关重要，现有指南依赖医疗记录、CTPA影像参数和血液检测，但血液检测在常规实践中常缺失。本文旨在研究仅凭医疗记录和CTPA影像提取的生物标志物能否准确进行风险分层。

Method: 基准测试了多种结合医疗记录、心脏生物标志物与肺血管信息的方法，包括向表格模型添加血管生物标志物，以及在血管树的固有图表示上应用图神经网络（GNNs）。使用私有数据集（n=353），该数据集具有唯一完整的肺栓塞风险分层数据。

Result: 在全局特征中，医疗记录和心脏生物标志物是最显著的预测因子，血管生物标志物未能进一步改善分层效果。更令人惊讶的是，基于血管图的GNN方法也未能超越基于全局特征的强表格基线模型。研究表明血管图可能不包含具有判别力的肺栓塞风险分层信息。

Conclusion: 对于肺栓塞风险分层任务，血管树的图结构特征可能不具有有效的判别信息，仅凭医疗记录和心脏生物标志物即可达到足够的分层性能。

Original Abstract: Risk stratification for pulmonary embolism (PE) is critical for clinical decision-making. Stratification guidelines are based on patient medical records, parameters measured from computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA), and blood tests. However, blood tests are often missing in routine practice. This work studies whether state-of-the-art models can accurately classify risk stratification from only medical records and biomarkers extracted from CTPA images. We benchmark different approaches to combine medical records and cardiac biomarkers with rich pulmonary vascular information; we add vascular biomarkers to tabular models and apply graph neural networks (GNNs) on the vascular tree's intrinsic graph representation. We use a private dataset (n=353) with uniquely complete data for PE risk stratification. Our results show that, among global features, medical records and cardiac biomarkers are the most significant predictors, while vascular biomarkers do not further improve stratification. Even more surprising, even GNNs on vascular graphs fail to outperform strong tabular baseline on global features. We consider hypotheses, on both models and data, that could explain this suboptimal performance. Our investigation suggests that, counter-intuitively, vascular graphs might hold no discriminative information for PE risk stratification. Code is available from https://github.com/creatis-myriad/GENESIS.

中文摘要翻译: 肺栓塞（PE）的风险分层对临床决策至关重要。分层指南基于患者病历、CT肺动脉造影（CTPA）测量的参数以及血液检测结果。然而，在常规临床实践中，血液检测结果常常缺失。本文研究最先进的模型是否能够仅凭病历和从CTPA图像中提取的生物标志物来准确进行风险分层分类。我们基准测试了多种将病历和心脏生物标志物与丰富的肺血管信息相结合的方法：我们将血管生物标志物添加到表格模型中，并在血管树的固有图表示上应用图神经网络（GNNs）。我们使用了一个私有数据集（n=353），该数据集具有唯一完整的肺栓塞风险分层数据。结果显示，在全局特征中，病历和心脏生物标志物是最显著的预测因子，而血管生物标志物未能进一步改善分层效果。更令人惊讶的是，即使基于血管图的GNN也无法超越基于全局特征的强表格基线模型。我们考虑了模型和数据两方面的假设来解释这种欠佳的性能。我们的研究表明，与直觉相反，血管图可能不包含对肺栓塞风险分层具有判别力的信息。代码可在 https://github.com/creatis-myriad/GENESIS 获取。

</details>


### [3] [Sol Video Inference Engine: Agent-Native Full-Stack Acceleration Framework for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23743)
*Yitong Li,Junsong Chen,Haopeng Li,Haozhe Liu,Jincheng Yu,Ligeng Zhu,Ping Luo,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个名为Sol Video Inference Engine的智能体驱动、无训练的视频扩散模型加速框架，通过组合缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化和核融合五种技术，针对特定部署场景自动优化，在多个模型上实现2倍以上加速且保持质量无损。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代视频扩散模型通过缩放提升生成质量，但推理成本也随之增加。最有效的加速策略高度依赖于具体实例（模型、硬件、推理配置的组合），不同模型在架构、数值敏感性和注意力分布上存在差异，不同硬件平台在内存层次、支持的数值格式和核吞吐量上各不相同，导致调优空间巨大，手动性能工程成本高昂。

Method: 提出Sol Video Inference Engine，一个智能体驱动的无训练加速框架。它组织五种广泛适用的技术（缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化和核融合）形成智能体加速栈。针对具体的部署目标（模型+硬件+服务配置），并行技能智能体优化每种技术的实现，智能体集成器将它们组合成全局加速栈，人类验证者提供生成质量反馈。

Result: 在三个不同规模和架构的视频模型（64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3、2B SANA-Video）上实例化该工作流，仅需很少的人力投入，完整加速栈实现了2倍以上的端到端加速，同时保持接近无损的VBench质量。

Conclusion: 该智能体框架有效地解决了视频扩散模型加速策略的实例特定性问题，通过自动化组合多种加速技术，在显著提升推理速度的同时保持了生成质量，验证了智能体驱动方法在视频扩散加速领域的有效性。

Original Abstract: Modern video diffusion models achieve higher generation quality through scaling, but this also increases inference cost. Although many acceleration methods have been proposed, a central challenge is that the most effective acceleration strategy is highly instance-specific: a recipe that works well for one combination of model, hardware, and inference configuration often does not transfer to another. Different models vary in architecture, numerical sensitivity, and attention concentration patterns. Inference settings differ in spatial and temporal resolution and video duration, while hardware platforms differ in memory hierarchy, supported numerical formats, and kernel throughput. These factors create a large tuning space, making manual performance engineering costly. We present Sol Video Inference Engine, an agentic, native, training-free acceleration framework for video diffusion models. It organizes five broadly applicable techniques, cache, sparse attention, token pruning, quantization, and kernel fusion, into an agentic acceleration stack for instance-specific optimization. For a concrete deployment target defined by a model, hardware platform, and serving configuration, parallel skill agents optimize the implementation of each technique, an agent integrator composes them into a global acceleration stack, and a human validator provides feedback on generation quality. We instantiate this workflow on three video models with different sizes and architectures: 64B Cosmos3-Super, 22B LTX-2.3, and 2B SANA-Video. With little human effort, the full stack achieves more than 2x end-to-end acceleration while maintaining near-lossless VBench quality, demonstrating the effectiveness of the agent framework for video diffusion acceleration.

中文摘要翻译: 现代视频扩散模型通过缩放实现了更高质量的生成，但这也增加了推理成本。尽管已经提出了许多加速方法，但一个核心挑战在于：最有效的加速策略高度依赖于具体实例——对一个模型、硬件和推理配置组合有效的方案，往往无法迁移到其他组合。不同模型在架构、数值敏感性和注意力集中模式上存在差异。推理设置在空间和时间分辨率以及视频时长上各不相同，而硬件平台在内存层次结构、支持的数值格式和核吞吐量方面也存在差异。这些因素构成了巨大的调优空间，使得手动性能工程成本高昂。我们提出了Sol Video Inference Engine，一个智能体驱动的、原生的、无需训练的加速框架，用于视频扩散模型。它将五种广泛适用的技术——缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化和核融合——组织成一个智能体加速栈，以实现实例特定的优化。对于由模型、硬件平台和服务配置定义的具体部署目标，并行技能智能体优化每种技术的实现，智能体集成器将它们组合成全局加速栈，人类验证者提供生成质量反馈。我们在三个不同规模和架构的视频模型上实例化了该工作流：64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3和2B SANA-Video。仅需很少的人力投入，完整加速栈实现了2倍以上的端到端加速，同时保持接近无损的VBench质量，证明了该智能体框架在视频扩散加速方面的有效性。

</details>


### [4] [Autonomous Video Generation with Counterfactual Controllability for Self-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24152)
*Xin Wang,Wenxuan Liu,Tongtong Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文批判了“视频生成即世界建模”的过度主张，指出视频生成模型仅学习部分隐式时空世界模型，而非完全可落地或可控的模型。文章提出以反事实可控性（counterfactual controllability）作为关键判据，认为具备该能力的自主视频生成是实现自演化世界模型的可行路径。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献声称视频生成本质上是世界建模，该观点虽推动了生成式AI走向时间延展的物理场景，但危险地依赖于“仅靠扩展视觉预测就能自动产生物理智能体”的信念。本文旨在纠正这一过度主张，提出更准确的表述并指出真正的瓶颈在于反事实可控性。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与概念辨析的方法，从反事实可控性（counterfactual controllability）这一判据出发，论证视频生成模型当前仅学习到部分隐式时空世界模型，缺乏对可控制变量、身体约束及有效未来的理解。提出以“自主视频生成+反事实可控性”作为实现自演化世界模型的新视角。

Result: 分析表明：模型可以生成合理的视频（如无人机穿越森林、机械臂操作杯子），却仍无法知道哪些变量可控、哪些约束属于特定身体、哪些未来在干预下仍然有效。这说明预测现实主义并非最前沿，反事实可控性才是关键判据。

Conclusion: 具备反事实可控性的自主视频生成是实现自演化世界模型的一条有前景的路径。核心判据应从预测真实性转向反事实可控性，即具备在动作下询问“会发生什么”的能力，以检验生成的未来能否通过身体约束，并将结果反馈回未来想象中。

Original Abstract: Existing literature claims that video generation essentially is world modelling. On the one hand, the claim is productive because it pushes generative AI beyond static images and toward temporally extended physical scenes. On the other hand, this claim dangerously relies on the belief that scaling visual prediction alone will automatically yield physical agents. We prefer a more accurate statement: video generation models learn a partial, implicit spatiotemporal world model, but not a fully grounded or controllable one. The reason is as follows: a model may generate a plausible video of a drone crossing a forest or a robot arm manipulating a cup, yet still fail to know which variables are controllable, which constraints belong to a particular body and which futures remain valid under intervention. The frontier in essence is not predictive realism alone, instead it emphasizes a self-evolving generative nature that requires the decisive criterion to be counterfactual controllability: the capability of asking what would happen under an action, to test whether the generated future can survive embodiment constraints and to feed the resulting action knowledge back into future imagination (generation). Therefore, in this paper we present a new perspective, i.e., autonomous video generation with counterfactual controllability is one promising way to realize self-evolving world models.

中文摘要翻译: 现有文献声称视频生成本质上就是世界建模。一方面，这一主张是有成效的，因为它推动生成式AI超越静态图像，走向时间延展的物理场景。另一方面，这一主张危险地依赖于"仅靠扩展视觉预测就能自动产生物理智能体"的信念。我们更倾向于一个更准确的表述：视频生成模型学习了一个部分的、隐式的时空世界模型，但并非一个完全可落地或可控的模型。原因如下：一个模型可以生成一段无人机穿越森林或机械臂操作杯子的合理视频，却仍然无法知道哪些变量是可控制的、哪些约束属于特定身体、以及哪些未来在干预下仍然有效。真正的瓶颈并非单纯的预测现实主义，而是强调一种自演化的生成本质，这要求以反事实可控性（counterfactual controllability）作为关键判据：即具备在某个动作下询问"会发生什么"的能力，以检验生成的未来能否经受住身体约束，并将由此产生的动作知识反馈回未来的想象（生成）中。因此，本文提出一个新的视角：具备反事实可控性的自主视频生成，是实现自演化世界模型的一条有前景的路径。

</details>


### [5] [MotifGen: Spatiotemporal interpolation of misaligned satellite images via multi-source generative modeling, in an application to tropical cyclones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24263)
*Clément Dauvilliers,Claire Monteleoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够处理多源、异质、非对齐地理空间数据的生成模型，用于热带气旋微波图像的时空插值。该模型通过自监督训练（随机屏蔽并重建一个数据源）结合红外与微波数据，在CRPS和功率谱指标上显著优于监督方法和确定性模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 微波卫星图像在监测热带气旋降水和强度方面至关重要，但其重访周期长，可能错过风暴的快速演化阶段。因此需要一种插值方法，然而不同仪器来源的微波数据高度异质（采样变化、时间间隔不规则、地理错位、仪器特性不同），现有方法难以处理这些复杂异构数据。

Method: 提出首个可应用于多源地理空间数据的生成模型。采用自监督训练策略：随机屏蔽一个数据源并重建该源。模型融合了多个微波仪器和红外观测数据，在非规则时间间隔下进行时空插值。

Result: 1) 自监督训练的CRPS显著低于监督训练；2) 融合红外与微波数据相比仅用微波数据有进一步提升；3) 生成模型的集合均值与确定性模型相当，而生成的功率谱显著更接近真实观测。

Conclusion: 这是首个能够在非规则时间间隔下结合多个微波仪器和红外观测对热带气旋微波图像进行插值的生成模型，有效解决了多源异构地理空间数据的融合问题。

Original Abstract: Microwave satellite imagery plays a crucial role in monitoring tropical cyclone precipitation and intensity worldwide, but suffers from long revisit times, potentially missing rapid storm evolution phases. While this raises the need for an interpolation method, it is made challenging by the high level of heterogeneity of microwave data coming from different instruments. In this work, we introduce the first generative model that can be applied to multiple geospatial sources that change across samples, occur at irregular time intervals, are misaligned geographically, and come from instruments with varying characteristics. We apply this model to the case of spatio-temporal interpolation of tropical cyclone microwave images from other microwave and infrared instruments. We train using a self-supervised task in which a random source is masked and reconstructed, and show that it leads to a significant decrease in Continuous Ranked Probability Score over supervised training. We show a further improvement by combining infrared and microwave data compared to microwave only. Using these improvements, the generative model produces an ensemble mean on par with that of a deterministic model, while generating a power spectrum significantly closer to that of true observations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first generative model that interpolates microwave images of cyclones by combining multiple microwave instruments and infrared observations at irregular time intervals.

中文摘要翻译: 微波卫星图像在全球范围内监测热带气旋降水和强度方面发挥着关键作用，但其重访周期较长，可能会错过风暴的快速演化阶段。这虽然凸显了插值方法的必要性，但来自不同仪器的微波数据高度异质，使得插值极具挑战性。本文提出了首个可应用于多源地理空间数据的生成模型，这些数据在样本间变化、时间间隔不规则、地理上未对齐，且来自具有不同特性的仪器。我们将该模型应用于热带气旋微波图像（结合其他微波和红外仪器数据）的时空插值任务。我们采用自监督训练策略：随机屏蔽一个数据源并重建该源，结果表明其连续等级概率评分（CRPS）显著低于监督训练。我们还发现，融合红外与微波数据相比仅使用微波数据能带来进一步提升。利用这些改进，生成模型的集合均值达到了与确定性模型相当的水平，同时生成的功率谱显著更接近真实观测。据我们所知，这是首个能够在非规则时间间隔下结合多个微波仪器和红外观测对气旋微波图像进行插值的生成模型。

</details>


### [6] [Open-Vocabulary BEV Segmentation with 3D-Aware Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24353)
*Hojun Choi,Seulbin Hwang,Dae Jung Kim,Kisung Kim,Hyunjung Shim,Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OVBEVSeg，一种几何感知的开放词汇BEV分割框架，通过高斯泼溅反投影和三级3D几何约束，在nuScenes上实现SOTA，未见类别mIoU领先闭集方法15.3，推理速度提升2.5倍，显存仅为投影方法的0.22倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有BEV感知方法局限于闭集场景，无法应对开放世界中的未知类别；直接将2D VLM语义提升到BEV存在严重的3D几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出OVBEVSeg框架，包含三个渐进阶段：(1) 2D-to-BEV伪标签，利用可靠3D投影实现开放词汇泛化；(2) 联合2D-BEV逐场景优化，引入BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性；(3) 3D几何蒸馏实现在线高效推理。核心采用高效高斯泼溅(GS)反投影机制。

Result: 在nuScenes上，未见类别mIoU超越闭集方法15.3；无新类GT标签时仍媲美使用40% GT标注的自/半监督基线；推理速度比投影法快2.5倍，显存仅为其0.22倍。

Conclusion: OVBEVSeq首次实现开放词汇BEV分割，通过几何感知的高斯泼溅框架有效解决了3D几何不一致问题，在精度、泛化能力和效率上均取得显著优势。

Original Abstract: Bird's-eye view (BEV) perception fuses multi-camera images into a unified top-down representation for autonomous driving. Despite recent progress, state-of-the-art methods remain confined to closed-set scenarios, making them vulnerable to unpredictable real-world environments. In this work, we introduce open-vocabulary BEV segmentation (OVBS), which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to recognize categories beyond the training set while maintaining precise BEV perception and real-time efficiency. A key challenge in OVBS lies in the 3D geometric inconsistency inherent in the ill-posed lifting of 2D VLM semantics into BEV. To address this, we propose OVBEVSeg, a geometry-aware OVBS framework that enhances efficient Gaussian splatting (GS)-based unprojection by leveraging robust 3D geometric constraints across three progressive stages: (1) 2D-to-BEV pseudo-labeling via reliable 3D projection for OV generalization; (2) joint 2D-BEV per-scene optimization with BEV structural constraints for 3D geometric consistency; and (3) 3D geometric distillation for online efficiency. On the nuScenes dataset, OVBEVSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming closed-set methods by 15.3 mIoU on unseen categories. Remarkably, even with no novel-class ground-truth labels, it remains competitive with self- and semi-supervised baselines trained with up to 40% of ground-truth annotations. Furthermore, it achieves 2.5x faster inference with only 0.22x the memory consumption of projection-based methods. Project page: https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/.

中文摘要翻译: 鸟瞰图(BEV)感知将多相机图像融合为统一的自顶向下表示，用于自动驾驶。尽管近期取得了进展，最先进的方法仍然局限于闭集场景，使其难以应对不可预测的现实环境。本文提出开放词汇BEV分割(OVBS)，利用视觉-语言模型(VLM)识别超出训练集的类别，同时保持精确的BEV感知和实时效率。OVBS的一个关键挑战在于将2D VLM语义提升至BEV这一不适定过程中固有的3D几何不一致性。为此，我们提出OVBEVSeg，一个几何感知的OVBS框架，通过利用三个渐进阶段的鲁棒3D几何约束来增强高效高斯泼溅(GS)反投影：(1)通过可靠3D投影进行2D到BEV伪标签，实现开放词汇泛化；(2)联合2D-BEV逐场景优化，引入BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性；(3)3D几何蒸馏实现在线高效推理。在nuScenes数据集上，OVBEVSeg取得了最先进性能，在未见类别上以15.3 mIoU超越闭集方法。值得注意的是，即使没有新类真值标签，它仍能与使用了多达40%真值标注的自监督和半监督基线相竞争。此外，其推理速度比基于投影的方法快2.5倍，显存消耗仅为其0.22倍。项目页面：https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/。

</details>


### [7] [MedPCFM: Improving Medical Point Cloud Completion by Integrating Point Transformers and Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24433)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于点云变换器v3（PTv3）的流匹配方法PCFM，用于医学点云补全，在颅骨缺损等数据集上实现了最先进的生成性能，且采样步数远少于扩散模型，并实现了高达7倍的吞吐量加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学点云补全对于解剖结构重建和下游临床工作流至关重要，但该领域的生成式建模研究尚不充分。

Method: 提出PCFM，一种基于PTv3的流匹配（flow matching）方法，用于医学点云补全；同时构建了确定性的PTv3编码器-解码器补全基线模型以及基于扩散的补全模型（PCDiff，分别使用PVCNN和PTv3作为去噪器）。

Result: PCFM与确定性PTv3基线竞争并实现了跨数据集的最先进生成性能，所需采样步数远少于扩散模型；在最佳运行点上，PTv3相比PVCNN骨干可为PCFM提供高达7倍的加速；通过改变模型大小和点数量的实验显示，更高点分辨率带来一致增益，不同模型尺度间存在信息量丰富的权衡。

Conclusion: 连续时间生成建模（流匹配）在医学点云补全任务上表现优异，PCFM在生成质量和效率之间取得了良好的平衡，PTv3骨干进一步带来了显著的吞吐量优势。

Original Abstract: Medical point cloud completion is important for anatomical reconstruction and downstream clinical workflows, yet generative modeling in this setting remains insufficiently studied. We investigate completion through continuous-time generative modeling and introduce PCFM, a PTv3-backed flow matching approach for medical point cloud completion. We evaluate on SkullFix and SkullBreak, and additionally on the more recent Mandibular Defect dataset. We build strong baselines by adapting PTv3 to a deterministic encoder-decoder completion model and by instantiating diffusion completion (PCDiff) with both PVCNN and PTv3 denoisers. PCFM with PTv3 is competitive with the deterministic PTv3 baseline and achieves state-of-the-art generative performance across datasets, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion. At the best operating points, PTv3 also yields clear throughput gains, providing up to a 7$\times$ speed-up for PCFM compared to a PVCNN backbone. Finally, we study empirical scaling trends by varying model size and point cardinality, showing consistent gains with higher point resolution and informative trade-offs across model scales.

中文摘要翻译: 医学点云补全对于解剖结构重建和下游临床工作流至关重要，然而该领域的生成式建模研究尚不充分。本文通过连续时间生成建模研究点云补全问题，提出了PCFM——一种基于PTv3的流匹配方法，用于医学点云补全。我们在SkullFix、SkullBreak以及更近期发布的Mandibular Defect数据集上进行评估。我们通过将PTv3适配为确定性编码器-解码器补全模型，以及使用PVCNN和PTv3去噪器实例化扩散补全模型（PCDiff），构建了强基线。基于PTv3的PCFM与确定性PTv3基线表现出竞争力，并在多个数据集上取得了最先进的生成性能，同时所需的采样步数远少于扩散模型。在最佳运行点上，PTv3还带来了显著的吞吐量提升，与PVCNN骨干相比，PCFM获得了高达7倍的加速。最后，我们通过改变模型大小和点数量来研究经验性的缩放趋势，结果表明更高点分辨率带来一致增益，且不同模型尺度之间存在信息量丰富的权衡。

</details>


### [8] [EERLoss: A Novel Loss Function for Training Deep Biometric Models. A Case Study in Keystroke Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24586)
*Nahuel Gonzalez,Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ivan DeAndres-Tame,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EERLoss，一种可微且任意精确的等错误率（EER）近似函数，用于训练深度生物识别模型。在击键动态验证这一高变异性行为生物特征任务上，该方法相比现有最优损失函数实现了约30%的相对EER降低，并显著加快了收敛速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习生物识别验证方法通常优化间接目标函数，导致优化过程与主要评估指标（等错误率，EER）之间存在不一致性。作者旨在消除这一偏差，直接优化任务相关的评估指标。

Method: 提出EERLoss，一种对EER的次可微、任意精确的近似函数；该框架还可通过调整适应DET曲线上任意特定工作点，提升泛化能力。

Result: 在大型KVC-onGoing基准（涵盖185,000+受试者）上的实验表明：（1）消融研究证实EERLoss优于现有最优损失函数；（2）收敛速度显著快于其他损失，降低训练成本；（3）使用EERLoss重新训练KVC-winning架构后，相对EER降低约30%，显著超越原始SoTA。

Conclusion: EERLoss作为一种任务对齐的训练目标，特别适用于高方差的生物特征模态，在大规模挑战性基准上验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Deep learning approaches to biometric verification are commonly trained by optimizing indirect objectives, creating a misalignment between the optimization process and the primary evaluation metric, typically the Equal Error Rate (EER). This paper introduces EERLoss: a subdifferentiable, arbitrarily accurate approximation to EER for training deep biometric models. Furthermore, this framework has the potential to be adapted to optimize any specific operating point on the DET curve, enhancing its generalizability. To validate this approach, EERLoss is evaluated on a particularly demanding behavioral biometric modality: keystroke dynamics verification. This task is characterized by its high intra-class and low inter-class variability. Experiments are conducted on the large-scale KVC-onGoing benchmark, incorporating data from over 185,000 subjects across different scenarios. A comprehensive ablation study initially demonstrates the superiority of EERLoss in comparison to existing state-of-the-art loss functions. It also converges substantially faster compared to other losses, reducing the overall training cost. Additionally, a comparison is made between the proposed loss and the KVC-winning architecture by re-training it with EERLoss, demonstrating that the proposed approach significantly outperforms the original SoTA, achieving a relative EER reduction of up to approx. 30\%. This improvement on a challenging, large-scale benchmark validates the effectiveness of EERLoss as a task-aligned training objective specifically suited for high-variance biometric traits.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习生物识别验证方法通常通过优化间接目标进行训练，这导致优化过程与主要评估指标（通常是等错误率EER）之间存在不一致。本文提出EERLoss：一种用于训练深度生物识别模型的、次可微且任意精确的EER近似函数。此外，该框架具有可适应性，能够优化DET曲线上任意特定工作点，从而增强其泛化能力。为了验证该方法，我们在一个极具挑战性的行为生物特征模态——击键动态验证上评估EERLoss。该任务的特点是类内变异性高而类间变异性低。实验在大规模KVC-onGoing基准上进行，涵盖来自不同场景的超过185,000名受试者的数据。一项全面的消融研究首先证明了EERLoss相较于现有最优损失函数的优越性。它还比其他损失函数收敛得更快，降低了整体训练成本。此外，我们将所提出的损失与KVC-winning架构进行了比较，通过使用EERLoss重新训练该架构，证明了所提出的方法显著优于原始SoTA，实现了高达约30%的相对EER降低。在具有挑战性的大规模基准上的这一改进，验证了EERLoss作为一种任务对齐的训练目标，特别适用于高方差生物特征模态的有效性。

</details>

### [9] [An iterative energy-based multimodal transformer for joint retrieval of wheat soil moisture, leaf area index, and plant height from Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25174)
*Shubham Kumar Singh,Peilei Fan,Suraj A. Yadav,Rajendra Prasad,Prashant K Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出迭代能量基Transformer (iEBT)，联合反演土壤水分、叶面积指数和植株高度三个作物-土壤耦合参数，利用Sentinel-1 SAR和Sentinel-2多光谱时序数据，通过学习能量函数迭代优化反演结果，在印度农田数据集上取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统前馈回归模型在异质性小农耕系统中难以有效处理雷达后向散射与光谱响应中土壤水分与冠层密度同时变化带来的歧义性，反演土壤水分、叶面积指数和植株高度仍是一个不适定逆问题。

Method: 提出迭代能量基Transformer (iEBT)，将多模态预测器嵌入共享序列，生成初始状态估计，然后通过归一化梯度下降迭代更新目标[SM, LAI, PH]向量，以最小化学习到的标量兼容性能量函数，实现联合反演。

Result: 在印度瓦拉纳西700个质控田间测量数据上，iEBT在随机测试集上达到最优学习模型性能，四种子平均R²=0.854±0.012（SM: 0.841, LAI: 0.905, PH: 0.821）。模态消融实验表明Sentinel-1驱动SM反演，Sentinel-2主导LAI，PH依赖两者联合特征。终端能量可作为非校准的后反演质量诊断，筛选最高10%能量样本可显著降低RMSE。

Conclusion: 尽管留一验证显示跨季节域迁移仍面临挑战，但兼容性引导的多模态融合为可靠的生物物理参数估计提供了一条结构化的自诊断路径。

Original Abstract: Field-scale retrieval of surface soil moisture (SM), leaf area index (LAI), and plant height (PH) is essential for precision agriculture, yet it remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Concurrent variations in soil moisture and canopy density generate substantial ambiguities in radar backscatter and spectral responses, which reduces the effectiveness of traditional feedforward regression models in heterogeneous smallholder cropping systems. This study presents the Iterative Energy-Based Transformer (iEBT) for the joint retrieval of coupled soil-canopy states from Sentinel-1 C-band SAR and Sentinel-2 multispectral time series. Instead of direct regression, iEBT embeds multi-modal predictors within a shared sequence, produces an initial state estimate, and iteratively updates the target [SM, LAI, PH] vector through normalized gradient descent to minimize a learned scalar compatibility energy function. Using 700 quality-controlled field measurements from Varanasi, India, iEBT achieved the highest learned-model performance on the random test split, with a four-seed mean R^2 of 0.854 \pm 0.012 (R_SM^2 = 0.841, R_LAI^2 = 0.905, R_PH^2 = 0.821). WCM and PROSAIL were retained as physically interpretable SAR and optical reference models for comparison. Modality ablations confirmed that Sentinel-1 drives SM retrieval, while Sentinel-2 dominates LAI, whereas PH relies on combined structural-phenological signatures. Crucially, the model's terminal energy functions as an uncalibrated post-retrieval quality diagnostic; screening the 10% highest-energy samples markedly reduced target level root-mean-square errors. While leave-one-campaign-out validation highlights persistent cross-season domain shift challenges due to localized management variations, compatibility-guided multimodal fusion offers a structured self-diagnostic path toward reliable biophysical parameter estimation

中文摘要翻译: 田间尺度的土壤水分、叶面积指数和植株高度的反演对于精准农业至关重要，但这仍然是一个不适定逆问题。土壤水分和冠层密度的同时变化会在雷达后向散射和光谱响应中产生显著的歧义性，这降低了传统前馈回归模型在异质性小农耕作系统中的有效性。本研究提出了迭代能量基Transformer，用于从Sentinel-1 C波段SAR和Sentinel-2多光谱时间序列联合反演耦合的土壤-冠层状态。与直接回归不同，iEBT将多模态预测器嵌入共享序列中，生成初始状态估计，并通过归一化梯度下降迭代更新目标[SM, LAI, PH]向量，以最小化学习到的标量兼容性能量函数。利用来自印度瓦拉纳西的700个质量控制的田间测量数据，iEBT在随机测试集上达到了最优的学习模型性能，四种子平均R²为0.854±0.012（SM: 0.841, LAI: 0.905, PH: 0.821）。保留WCM和PROSAIL作为物理可解释的SAR和光学参考模型进行比较。模态消融实验证实，Sentinel-1驱动SM反演，Sentinel-2主导LAI，而PH依赖于两者结合的结构-物候特征。关键的是，模型的终端能量可作为非校准的后反演质量诊断工具；筛选能量最高的10%样本显著降低了目标级别的均方根误差。虽然留一验证实验揭示了由于局部管理差异而存在的持久的跨季节域迁移挑战，但兼容性引导的多模态融合为可靠的生物物理参数估计提供了一条结构化的自诊断路径。

</details>


### [10] [Systematic Exploration of 4-Expert Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts via Automated Pipeline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23739)
*Yashkumar R Lukhi,Harsh Rameshbhai Moradiya,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LEMUR神经网络数据集生态系统中异质4专家混合专家（MoE4）架构的自动化大规模搜索流水线。该流水线将手动设计替换为确定性代码组装生成器，在28天RTX 4090上生成4,463个候选模型，并发现由于itertools.combinations的字母顺序枚举导致搜索空间被锚定在AirNet单一族上（仅覆盖4.8%的理论组合）。在该有偏范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3组合产生最高精度（均值达0.632），FractalNet和MNASNet被判定为低效族。流水线、分析工具和修正后的生成器已在NNGPT开源项目中发布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 手工设计的异质MoE架构效率低下且难以大规模探索，需要一种自动化的方法系统性地组合LEMUR数据库中的基础架构族，以发现高性能的MoE4集成模型。

Method: 构建确定性代码组装生成器，将LEMUR数据库中的基础架构族系统组合为MoE4集成，每个集成由带温度缩放的卷积门控网络控制，并采用mixup增强和余弦退火学习率调度，在RTX 4090上运行28天进行大规模搜索。

Result: 生成4,463个候选模型（197批次），其中1,021个成功评估；发现搜索空间因itertools.combinations的字母顺序枚举而被锚定在AirNet族上（仅覆盖理论组合的4.8%）；在AirNet锚定范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3组合平均精度最高（0.632），FractalNet和MNASNet为低效族。

Conclusion: 识别了搜索空间覆盖偏差的根本原因（字母枚举排序），提出了分层随机采样修复方案，并建议在未来的搜索中排除FractalNet和MNASNet以提高效率。流水线和分析工具已开源。

Original Abstract: We present an automated large-scale search pipeline for heterogeneous 4-Expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE4) architectures within the LEMUR neural network dataset ecosystem. Building on a hand-crafted heterogeneous MoE reference model, we replace manual design with a deterministic code-assembly generator that systematically combines base architecture families drawn from the LEMUR database into MoE4 ensembles, each governed by a convolutional gating network with temperature scaling, mixup augmentation, and cosine-annealed learning rate scheduling. Over a 28-day campaign on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the pipeline generated 4,463 candidate models across 197 batches, of which 1,021 were evaluated successfully. A critical finding emerged from the campaign: due to alphabetical enumeration via itertools.combinations, the entire explored search space (4.8% of the theoretical 23,751 possible 4-family combinations) is anchored to a single family, AirNet. We characterise this coverage bias precisely, identify the root cause in the generator, and propose a stratified random sampling fix. Within the AirNet anchored scope, ShuffleNet and MobileNetV3 consistently co-produce the highest-accuracy ensembles (mean accuracy up to 0.632), while FractalNet and MNASNet are identified as low-yield families warranting exclusion in future campaigns. The pipeline, analysis artefacts, and corrected generator are released as part of the open-source NNGPT project at https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种针对LEMUR神经网络数据集生态系统中异质4专家混合专家（MoE4）架构的自动化大规模搜索流水线。在手工构建的异质MoE参考模型基础上，我们将手动设计替换为确定性代码组装生成器，该生成器系统地将LEMUR数据库中的基础架构族组合为MoE4集成，每个集成由带有温度缩放的卷积门控网络、mixup增强和余弦退火学习率调度控制。在一块NVIDIA RTX 4090上进行的28天搜索活动中，该流水线生成了197批共4,463个候选模型，其中1,021个成功完成评估。该活动揭示了一个关键发现：由于通过itertools.combinations进行字母顺序枚举，整个已探索的搜索空间（仅占理论上23,751种可能的4族组合的4.8%）被锚定在单一族AirNet上。我们精确描述了这种覆盖偏差，定位了生成器中的根本原因，并提出了分层随机采样修复方案。在AirNet锚定范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3一致地共同产出最高精度的集成（平均精度高达0.632），而FractalNet和MNASNet被判定为低效族，建议在未来搜索中排除。该流水线、分析工件和修正后的生成器已作为开源NNGPT项目的一部分发布，地址为https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt。

</details>


### [11] [Weight-Space Geometry of Offline Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23740)
*Aleksandr Nikolich,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Karina Romanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多种分析工具（余弦相似度、主角子空间分析、线性模式连通性、CKA）系统比较了六种离线强化学习损失（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）在相同数学推理数据下的权重更新机制差异，发现多数方法（SFT、RFT、RIFT）权重更新几乎共线，而DFT、Offline GRPO和DPO各有不同的正交分量，其中DPO达到了最高准确率但位于几乎正交的子空间中。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究仅用下游准确率比较各种离线强化学习损失函数，但缺乏对这些损失函数在机制层面是否不同、是否收敛到相似权重更新的深入理解。作者希望揭示不同损失函数在权重更新方向上的本质差异。

Method: 在相同的数学推理数据（GSM8K和AIME）上，基于同一基座模型（Qwen3-4B）使用仅注意力的LoRA微调，训练六种方法（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）。通过余弦相似度、主角子空间分析、线性模式连通性和CKA（Centered Kernel Alignment）四种工具分析权重更新的机械差异。

Result: (i) SFT、RFT和RIFT的权重增量几乎共线（余弦≥0.97，主角中位数~7°），GSM8K准确率相当（87-88%）；(ii) DFT虽使用相同数据，但其方向偏离程度超过任何奖励加权方法；(iii) Offline GRPO在SFT方向上添加了显著正交分量（全局~67%，深层最高~86%），但仍处于SFT损失盆地中；(iv) DPO位于近乎正交的子空间，存在模式连通性障碍，晚期层CKA降至~0.46，但达到最高准确率（GSM8K 93.5%，AIME26 30.0%），不过其学习率比其他方法小10倍。

Conclusion: 不同离线强化学习损失函数在权重更新机制上存在本质差异，并非机械等价。SFT/RFT/RIFT近乎等价的更新方向解释了其相似性能；DPO虽性能最佳但更新方向正交，且其优势部分源于学习率差异，需要进一步控制变量研究。

Original Abstract: Offline reinforcement-learning losses (RFT, RIFT, DFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) are widely used to distill reasoning from large teachers into smaller students, and are typically compared on downstream accuracy alone. We ask whether they are mechanistically distinct or converge to a similar weight update. Training six methods (SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) on identical math rollouts from a single base model (Qwen3-4B) with attention-only LoRA, we analyze the resulting deltas via cosine similarity, principal-angle subspace analysis, linear mode connectivity, and CKA. We observe: (i) SFT, RFT, and RIFT have nearly colinear weight deltas (cosine >= 0.97, top-1 principal angle ~7 deg median over 144 modules) and comparable GSM8K accuracy (87-88%, n=1319; pairwise McNemar p >= 0.15); (ii) DFT diverges further in direction than any reward-weighted method despite using the same data; (iii) Offline GRPO adds a substantial component orthogonal to the SFT direction (~67% globally, up to ~86% in late layers) while staying in the SFT loss basin; (iv) DPO sits in a near-orthogonal subspace, shows a mode-connectivity barrier, and collapses late-layer CKA to ~0.46. DPO also reaches the highest accuracy in our protocol on both GSM8K (93.5%, McNemar p < 10^-9 vs. each other method) and AIME26 (30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%); its training uses a 10x smaller learning rate than the others (the standard convention), so the update-norm and accuracy gaps reflect loss-function and optimizer choices jointly, and a learning-rate-matched DPO comparison is left for future work.

中文摘要翻译: 离线强化学习损失（RFT、RIFT、DFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）被广泛用于将大教师模型的推理能力蒸馏到较小的学生模型中，且通常仅通过下游准确率进行比较。我们探究这些损失函数在机制上是否存在差异，抑或收敛到相似的权重更新。在相同基座模型（Qwen3-4B）上使用仅注意力的LoRA，对六种方法（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）在相同的数学推理数据上进行训练，我们通过余弦相似度、主角子空间分析、线性模式连通性和CKA分析所得的权重增量。我们观察到：(i) SFT、RFT和RIFT的权重增量几乎共线（余弦≥0.97，144个模块的主角中位数约7°），GSM8K准确率相当（87-88%，n=1319；成对McNemar检验p≥0.15）；(ii) 尽管使用相同数据，DFT的方向偏离程度超过任何奖励加权方法；(iii) Offline GRPO在SFT方向上添加了显著的正交分量（全局约67%，深层最高约86%），但仍处于SFT损失盆地中；(iv) DPO位于近乎正交的子空间，表现出模式连通性障碍，且晚期层CKA降至约0.46。DPO在我们的实验方案中达到了最高准确率（GSM8K 93.5%，成对McNemar检验p<10⁻⁹ vs. 其他每种方法；AIME26 30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%）；其训练使用的学习率比其他方法小10倍（标准惯例），因此权重更新范数和准确率差距共同反映了损失函数和优化器选择的影响，学习率匹配的DPO对比留待未来工作。

</details>


### [12] [A Survey on Federated Causal Discovery and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23741)
*Xianjie Guo,Yuwei Wang,Guodu Xiang,Xiaoli Tang,Kui Yu,Han Yu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦因果推理领域，通过多维度分类法（方法范式、联邦拓扑、结构范围）对联邦因果发现（FCD）和联邦因果推断（FCI）进行了全面组织，并首次将两者形式化为统一的联邦因果推理流水线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联邦因果发现与推断领域跨学科性强，但缺乏系统性综述，给研究者带来入门障碍。现有工作通常将FCD和FCI分开处理，未揭示二者作为统一因果推理流水线中互补阶段的内在联系。

Method: 基于三个核心设计决策（结构如何学习、数据如何划分、各方获得何种结构知识），沿三个维度（方法范式、联邦拓扑、结构范围）组织FCD；按目标估计量（平均 vs. 个性化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（从经典加权到现代深度生成架构）对FCI进行分类；形式化FCD与FCI作为统一联邦因果推理流水线的互补阶段。

Result: 系统梳理了FCD和FCI的各类方法、关键实践维度（时间动态、数据异质性、缺失数据、非一致变量集），并指出了隐私、通信效率、理论保证和应用领域等共享关注点及未来开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文通过多维度分类法填补了联邦因果推理领域的综述空白，建立了FCD与FCI的统一框架，明确了当前研究挑战和未来方向。

Original Abstract: Causal reasoning, which encompasses the discovery of causal structures and the inference of causal effects, is fundamental to data-driven decision making. In practice, data for reliable causal analysis are often distributed across institutions and cannot be centralized due to privacy regulations or communication constraints. Federated learning (FL) addresses this by enabling collaborative analysis without raw data sharing, giving rise to the rapidly growing field of federated causal discovery (FCD) and inference (FCI). However, the interdisciplinary nature of this field and the absence of a comprehensive survey present barriers to entry for researchers. This paper bridges that gap by providing a systematic review through multi-dimensional taxonomies. Grounded in the three core design decisions underlying any FCD solution, namely how structures are learned, how data are partitioned, and what structural knowledge each party obtains, we organize FCD along three axes: methodological paradigm, federation topology, and structural scope. We further examine key practical dimensions, including temporal dynamics, data heterogeneity, missing data, and non-identical variable sets. For FCI, we categorize methods by target estimand (average versus individualized/conditional treatment effects) and by estimation strategy, from classical weighting methods to modern deep generative architectures. Unlike prior works that treat FCD and FCI separately, we formalize their connection as complementary stages of a unified federated causal reasoning pipeline, where FCD supplies the structural knowledge required for valid effect estimation in FCI. Finally, we highlight their shared concerns regarding privacy, communication efficiency, theoretical guarantees, and application domains, and conclude by identifying open challenges for future research.

中文摘要翻译: 因果推理涵盖因果结构的发现与因果效应的推断，是数据驱动决策的基础。在实践中，可靠因果分析所需的数据通常分布于多个机构，且因隐私规定或通信限制而无法集中。联邦学习（FL）通过支持无需原始数据共享的协作分析解决了这一问题，催生了联邦因果发现（FCD）与推断（FCI）这一快速发展的领域。然而，该领域的跨学科性质以及缺乏系统性综述给研究者带来了入门障碍。本文通过多维分类法进行全面系统综述，弥合了这一空白。基于任何FCD解决方案背后的三个核心设计决策——即结构如何学习、数据如何划分、以及各方获得何种结构知识——我们从方法范式、联邦拓扑和结构范围三个维度组织FCD。我们进一步考察了关键实践维度，包括时间动态、数据异质性、缺失数据和非一致变量集。对于FCI，我们按照目标估计量（平均处理效应与个性化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（从经典加权方法到现代深度生成架构）进行分类。与以往将FCD和FCI分开处理的工作不同，我们将两者之间的联系形式化为统一联邦因果推理流水线中的互补阶段：FCD为FCI中的有效效应估计提供所需的结构知识。最后，我们强调了它们在隐私、通信效率、理论保证和应用领域方面的共同关注点，并总结指出了未来研究的开放挑战。

</details>


### [13] [Low-power analogue neural networks with trainable nonlinear connections for continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23742)
*Ian T. Vidamour,Fernando Aguirre,Thomas J. Hayward,Matthew O. A. Ellis,Charles Swindells,Alexander McDonnell,Martin Trefzer,Finley Robins,Luca Manneschi,Susan Stepney,Tony Kenyon,Oliver J. Sutton,Jack C. Gartside,Ivan Y. Tyukin,Adnan Mehonic,Eleni Vasilaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络启发，在物理神经网络的连接上放置可训练非线性函数（实现为模拟带通滤波器），相比多层感知机在光滑连续值任务上能以更少节点和连接实现高效表征，但在分类任务上无参数效率优势；跨约35,000个连接验证了硬件迁移保真度，专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物理神经网络大多将非线性器件响应强制作为标量权重，未能充分利用模拟物理计算的能力。本文旨在通过在连接上放置可训练非线性函数，使每个物理连接成为可学习的计算单元，从而提升物理神经网络的表征效率。

Method: 借鉴Kolmogorov-Arnold网络思想，将可训练的非线性函数置于物理神经网络的连接上，并在现场可编程模拟阵列上将函数实现为模拟带通滤波器。同时探索了基于CMOS和忆阻器的硬件实现方案。

Result: 1) 在光滑连续值任务（机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点追踪）上，该网络以远少于多层感知机的节点和连接实现高效表征；2) 在分类类决策边界任务上无参数效率优势；3) 训练后网络可跨约35,000个连接以量化保真度迁移到硬件；4) 专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦；5) 忆阻器实现仿真中复现了相同行为。

Conclusion: 优势源于将可训练非线性置于连接上这一设计理念，而非特定的物理器件实现。物理基函数的平滑性是决定任务收益的关键因素。

Original Abstract: Physical neural networks promise low-power machine learning by computing directly with analogue device physics, but most architectures force nonlinear device responses to act as scalar weights. Inspired by Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, we place trainable nonlinear functions on the connections, making each physical connection a learnable computational element. Realising these functions as analogue band-pass filters on field-programmable analogue arrays, we find that the benefit is task-dependent and follows from the smoothness of the physical basis: the networks represent smooth, continuously valued targets, including robotic kinematics, continuous control, and photovoltaic maximum-power-point tracking, with far fewer nodes and connections than multilayer perceptrons, but offer no parameter-efficiency advantage on classification-like decision boundaries. Trained networks transfer to hardware across approximately 35,000 connections with quantified fidelity, and a dedicated CMOS implementation is projected to operate at approximately 30 microwatts. A memristive realisation reproduces the same behaviour in simulation, indicating that the advantage comes from placing trainable nonlinearity on connections, rather than from a particular device.

中文摘要翻译: 物理神经网络通过直接利用模拟器件物理进行计算，有望实现低功耗机器学习，但大多数架构强制将非线性器件响应作为标量权重使用。受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络启发，我们将可训练的非线性函数置于连接上，使每个物理连接成为一个可学习的计算单元。在现场可编程模拟阵列上将这些函数实现为模拟带通滤波器后，我们发现其优势与任务相关，并源于物理基函数的平滑性：该网络在表征光滑的连续值目标（包括机器人运动学、连续控制和光伏最大功率点追踪）时，所需的节点和连接数远少于多层感知机，但在分类类决策边界任务上并无参数效率优势。训练后的网络可跨约35,000个连接以量化保真度迁移到硬件，且专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦。忆阻器实现在仿真中复现了相同行为，表明该优势源于将可训练非线性置于连接上，而非特定的器件。

</details>


### [14] [Synergizing Physically Constrained MCMC and Chemical-Informed Gaussian Processes for Reaction Network Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23757)
*Runzhe Liu,Zihao Wang,Wenbo Yang,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合尖峰-板拓扑采样、硬约束筛选和化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型的灰箱工作流PC-MCMC-CIGP，用于从稀疏有噪化学时序数据中提取可解释的控制方程，并在H2+Br2基准和苯乙烯环氧化反应上验证了其有效性和优越性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从稀疏且有噪声的化学时序数据中提取可解释的控制方程仍然困难，因为离散反应拓扑与连续动力学参数紧密耦合，现有方法难以兼顾可解释性与预测精度。

Method: PC-MCMC-CIGP灰箱工作流，包含：（1）尖峰-板拓扑采样（spike-and-slab topology sampling）；（2）硬性守恒与热力学筛选（hard conservation and thermodynamic screening）；（3）化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型用于参数校准与实验设计。

Result: 在H2+Br2基准上，约束采样器能够区分基本自由基路径与欺骗性唯象拟合；在苯乙烯环氧化反应上，CIGP优化循环相比GP-BO基线使最终产率提升12.5%；10-seed采集策略研究表明，PC-EI显著减少低产率BO建议，而EI类准则获得最强的最终产率表现。

Conclusion: PC-MCMC-CIGP通过物理约束的组件集成与显式不确定性感知的采集选择，为从稀疏有噪化学数据中学习可解释控制方程提供了可复现的有效方案。

Original Abstract: Extracting interpretable governing equations from sparse, noisy chemical time-series data remains difficult because discrete reaction topology and continuous kinetic parameters are tightly coupled. We present PC-MCMC-CIGP, a reproducible gray-box workflow that combines spike-and-slab topology sampling, hard conservation and thermodynamic screening, and a Chemical-Informed Gaussian Process (CIGP) residual model for parameter calibration and experimental design. The methodological contribution is not a new MCMC or GP family in isolation; rather, it is the integration of these components into a physically constrained workflow with explicit uncertainty-aware acquisition choices. On the H2 + Br2 benchmark, the constrained sampler distinguishes elementary radical pathways from deceptive phenomenological fits in our experiments. On styrene epoxidation, the CIGP optimization loop improves final yield by 12.5% over the reported GP-BO baseline. A new 10-seed acquisition study shows that EI, GWU, PC-EI, uncertainty sampling, discrepancy hunting, and random search have different trade-offs: PC-EI substantially reduces low-yield BO suggestions, while EI-style criteria give the strongest final-yield performance.

中文摘要翻译: 从稀疏且有噪声的化学时序数据中提取可解释的控制方程仍然具有挑战性，因为离散反应拓扑结构与连续动力学参数紧密耦合。我们提出了PC-MCMC-CIGP，这是一个可复现的灰箱工作流，结合了尖峰-板拓扑采样、硬性守恒与热力学筛选，以及用于参数校准和实验设计的化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型。本文的方法贡献并非单独提出新的MCMC或GP族模型，而是将这些组件整合为一个物理约束的工作流，并带有显式的不确定性感知采集选择。在H2+Br2基准测试中，约束采样器在我们的实验中能够区分基本自由基路径与欺骗性的唯象拟合。在苯乙烯环氧化反应中，CIGP优化循环相比已报道的GP-BO基线将最终产率提升了12.5%。一项新的10-seed采集研究表明，EI、GWU、PC-EI、不确定性采样、差异搜索和随机搜索具有不同的权衡：PC-EI显著减少了低产率的贝叶斯优化建议，而EI类准则在最终产率表现上最为强劲。

</details>


### [15] [Exploring Dualistic Meta-Learning to Enhance Domain Generalization in Open Set Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23758)
*Xiran Wang,Jian Zhang,Lei Qi,Yang Gao,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为MEDIC的双重元学习策略，通过联合域-类匹配的隐式梯度对齐，同时处理域间和类间任务划分，在开放集域泛化中实现平衡的决策边界，既识别未知类又不过度拒绝已知类。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放集域泛化方法采用一对多分类器时，正负样本数量严重不平衡（少数正样本 vs 大量负样本），导致决策边界偏向正样本，模型会过度拒绝分布外数据（包括来自未知域的已知类样本），影响泛化性能。

Method: 提出一种名为MEDIC（Dualistic MEta-learning with joint DomaIn-Class matching）的双重元学习策略，同时考虑域间和类间任务拆分的隐式梯度匹配，以找到对域和类都平衡的最优决策边界。

Result: MEDIC不仅在开放集场景下优于现有方法，而且保持了具有竞争力的闭集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过联合考虑域匹配和类匹配的元学习策略，可以有效解决开放集域泛化中因标签不匹配和类别不平衡导致的过度拒绝问题，实现域和类两个维度上的平衡决策边界。

Original Abstract: Domain generalization learns from multiple source domains to generalize to unseen target domains. However, it often neglects the realistic case of label mismatch between source and target. Open set domain generalization is then proposed to recognize unseen classes in unseen domains. A simple approach trains one-vs-all classifiers to separate each class and detect outliers as unknown. Yet, the imbalance between few positive samples and many negative samples skews the decision boundary towards the positive ones, leading the model to over-reject out-of-distribution data, even from known classes in unseen domains. In this paper, we propose a novel meta-learning stategy called dualistic MEta-learning with joint DomaIn-Class matching (MEDIC), which considers implicit gradient matching towards inter-domain and inter-class task splits simultaneously to find optimal boundaries balanced for both domains and classes. Experimental results show that MEDIC not only outperforms prior methods in open set scenarios, but also maintains competitive close set generalization ability.

中文摘要翻译: 域泛化从多个源域中学习，以泛化到未见过的目标域。然而，它常常忽略了源域和目标域之间标签不匹配的现实情况。开放集域泛化因此被提出，旨在识别未见域中的未见类别。一种简单的方法训练一对多分类器来分离每个类别并检测离群点作为未知类。然而，少数正样本与大量负样本之间的不平衡使得决策边界偏向正样本，导致模型过度拒绝分布外数据，甚至包括来自未见域中已知类的数据。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的元学习策略，称为双重元学习与联合域-类匹配（MEDIC），该策略同时考虑域间和类间任务拆分的隐式梯度匹配，以找到对域和类都平衡的最优决策边界。实验结果表明，MEDIC不仅在开放集场景下优于现有方法，而且保持了具有竞争力的闭集泛化能力。

</details>


### [16] [One Ruler: A Same-Hands Re-Evaluation of Bivariate Causal Direction on Tuebingen, with a Parameter-Free Compression Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23767)
*Wietse Stienstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的评估协议重新评测了Tübingen因果对数据集上的多种方法，发现文献中广泛引用的准确率因评测协议不同而被高估。一个零参数的排序条件压缩基线（bz2）在统一标准下达到74.7%的加权准确率，与最强方法相当。论文揭示了通过测试集模型选择和显著性门控弃权机制夸大结果的普遍问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 文献中报告的头条准确率来自不同作者的不同协议（不同子集、权重、模型选择和决策率），导致不可比较。作者认为这是错误的比较方式，需要在同一标准下公平地重新评估所有方法。

Method: 提出一个故意最小化的基线方法——排序条件压缩（sorted-conditional compression）：对数据进行量化、排序、一阶差分处理，然后输入现成的bz2压缩器，该模型零可调参数。同时设计严格统一的评估协议：全部102个相同的数据对，强制对所有对做出决策，不允许调参。

Result: 基线方法达到74.7%加权准确率（p=3.7e-7）；在SLOPE测试的100个数据对上达到76.0%，与SLOPE强制决策结果（77.2%）差距仅1.2个百分点（McNemar p=0.39）；RECI复现结果为70.7%（非文献常引用的77.5%，后者源于抄错单元格）；SLOPE公布的82.4%是基于其显著性测试选择后的子集。统一评估下所有方法聚集在70-75%区间，零参数压缩器与最强方法持平。

Conclusion: 文献中广泛引用的高准确率因评测协议差异而被系统性高估；压缩分数大小是无模型混淆标志（p=2.8e-68）；预注册的证伪测试以有启发性的方式失败，限制了该方法的理论解释范围。

Original Abstract: Headline accuracies on the Tuebingen cause-effect pairs are routinely compared across papers even though each is measured under its authors' own protocol -- different pair subsets, weightings, model-selection, and decision rates. We argue this is the wrong comparison and run the right one: a same-hands re-evaluation in which every method is run by us on the identical 102 pairs, with one strict rule -- no tuning and a decision forced on every pair. As a clean reference point we introduce a deliberately minimal baseline: sorted-conditional compression, which feeds quantized, sorted, first-differenced data to an off-the-shelf compressor (bz2) and has zero fitted parameters. Under the common ruler the ranking differs sharply from the literature. Our baseline reaches 74.7% weighted accuracy (p = 3.7e-7); on the same 100 pairs that SLOPE is evaluated on it scores 76.0%, a 1.2-point gap below the authors' own forced-decision SLOPE (77.2%) that is well inside noise (McNemar p = 0.39). A faithful re-run of RECI lands at 70.7% -- inside the original authors' reported error bar, not the 77.5% often quoted (which we trace to a mis-copied cell). SLOPE's published 82.4% is a decided-subset figure: scoring the authors' own stored output only on the pairs its significance test chose to answer reproduces 81.7%. Under the common ruler the methods cluster in the low-to-mid 70s and the zero-parameter compressor ties the strongest of them. We document the mechanisms that inflate published figures (test-set model selection, significance-gated abstention) and contribute two further results: compression score magnitude is a model-free confounding flag (p = 2.8e-68), and a pre-registered falsification test fails in an instructive way that bounds the method's theoretical interpretation. Code, pre-registrations, and per-pair outputs are released.

中文摘要翻译: 图宾根因果对数据集上的头条准确率经常被跨论文比较，尽管每篇论文都是在作者自己的协议下测量的——不同的数据对子集、权重、模型选择和决策率。我们认为这是错误的比较方式，并进行了正确的比较：一次同平台的重新评估，由我们对相同的102个数据对运行每种方法，遵循一条严格规则——不调参，并对每个数据对强制做出决策。作为干净的参考点，我们引入了一个故意最小化的基线：排序条件压缩，它将量化、排序、一阶差分后的数据输入现成的压缩器（bz2），且零可调参数。在统一标尺下，排名与文献中的结果大相径庭。我们的基线达到74.7%的加权准确率（p=3.7e-7）；在与SLOPE评估相同的100个数据对上，它得分为76.0%，与作者自己强制决策的SLOPE（77.2%）之间仅差1.2个百分点，完全在噪声范围内（McNemar p=0.39）。忠实复现的RECI得分为70.7%——在原作者报告的错误范围内，而非经常引用的77.5%（我们发现源自一个抄错的单元格）。SLOPE公布的82.4%是一个决策子集数据：仅在其显著性测试选择回答的数据对上评分作者自己存储的输出，复现结果为81.7%。在统一标尺下，各方法聚集在70到75百分比的低位区间，而零参数压缩器与其中最强的方法持平。我们记录了导致公开数据膨胀的机制（测试集模型选择、显著性门控弃权），并贡献了另外两个结果：压缩分数大小是一个无模型混淆标志（p=2.8e-68），以及一个预注册的证伪测试以有启发性的方式失败，限制了该方法的理论解释范围。代码、预注册和每个数据对的结果均已发布。

</details>


### [17] [Deciphering Fingerprints of 3D Molecular Surfaces for Accurate Epitope Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23830)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi,Li Erran Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurfBind是一种基于分子表面表征的表位预测框架，利用Transformer架构整合几何与物理化学信息，在SAbDab和DB5.5基准上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖序列或骨架结构，难以捕捉不连续的、表面驱动的表位，因此需要一种直接基于分子表面进行表位预测的框架。

Method: SurfBind采用基于Transformer的架构，通过面片级表面建模、结合物感知的交叉注意力机制以及层次化从粗到细的预测范式，直接在分子表面表征上整合几何和物理化学线索。

Result: 在SAbDab和DB5.5等挑战性表位识别基准上，SurfBind达到了最先进的性能，并在未见抗体和构象状态上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 交互感知的表面建模对于理解蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用的关键机制具有重要价值，SurfBind证明了表面中心学习框架在表位预测中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Molecular surfaces encode the geometric and physicochemical patterns that determine antibody-antigen recognition, central to epitope prediction. However, existing methods rely on sequences or backbone structures and struggle to capture discontinuous, surface-driven epitopes. This study presents SurfBind, a surface-centric learning framework for epitope prediction that operates directly on molecular surface representations. SurfBind integrates geometric and physicochemical cues through a Transformer-based architecture with patch-level surface modeling, binder-aware cross-attention, and a hierarchical coarse-to-fine prediction paradigm. Experiments on challenging epitope identification benchmarks, including SAbDab and DB5.5, demonstrate that SurfBind achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization across unseen antibodies and conformational states, highlighting the value of interaction-aware surface modeling for understanding the crucial mechanisms of protein-protein interactions.

中文摘要翻译: 分子表面编码了决定抗体-抗原识别的几何和物理化学模式，而这一识别过程是表位预测的核心。然而，现有方法依赖于序列或骨架结构，难以捕捉不连续的、表面驱动的表位。本研究提出了SurfBind，一种直接基于分子表面表征进行表位预测的表面中心学习框架。SurfBind通过基于Transformer的架构，结合面片级表面建模、结合物感知的交叉注意力机制以及层次化从粗到细的预测范式，整合了几何和物理化学线索。在包括SAbDab和DB5.5在内的挑战性表位识别基准上的实验表明，SurfBind达到了最先进的性能，并在未见抗体和构象状态上展现出强大的泛化能力，凸显了交互感知的表面建模对于理解蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用关键机制的重要价值。

</details>


### [18] [Reconstructing GRACE Terrestrial Water Storage with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks: An Application to South America](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23833)
*Lukas Arzoumanidis,Lara Johannsen,Klara Middendorf,Annette Eicker,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图神经网络（MTGNN）的方法，利用ERA5再分析数据和GRACE卫星观测，将陆地水储量异常（TWSA）重建至1940年，在保持高精度的同时大幅减少所需预测因子数量，并开源实现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GRACE和GRACE-FO卫星任务仅从2002年开始提供TWS变化数据，时间跨度不足以支持长时间尺度的气候分析，因此需要将TWSA记录向前重建至更早时期。

Method: 采用多变量时间序列图神经网络（MTGNN），学习每日ERA5气象强迫（降水、蒸散发、径流）与月度GRACE观测之间的关系；构建静态可解释的混合邻接矩阵，结合测地邻近性和气候时间序列的滞后相关性，以编码空间依赖关系。

Result: 网格单元皮尔逊相关系数0.69，流域均值相关系数0.94，近零偏差；成功再现2015/16厄尔尼诺和2020/21拉尼娜事件的空间特征；与现有方法相比，达到相当精度但仅需约1/10至1/2的预测因子；所有模型在干旱区域均存在特征性弱点。

Conclusion: 基于图神经网络的重建方法在统计性能上与现有最优方法相当，但显著降低了模型复杂度（更少的预测因子），并揭示了现有模型在干旱区域的共性问题；完整实现已开源。

Original Abstract: Terrestrial water storage (TWS) integrates snow, soil moisture, surface water, and groundwater and is a key indicator of how climate variability and human activity reshape the global water cycle. The GRACE and GRACE-FO satellite missions provide the only direct, globally consistent observations of TWS change, but their record only begins in 2002 which is too short for many climate-scale analyses. We present a deep learning application that reconstructs monthly GRACE-like TWS anomalies (TWSA) back to 1940 by learning the relationship between daily ERA5 meteorological forcing (precipitation, evapotranspiration, runoff) and monthly GRACE observations. In contrast to prior reconstruction approaches based on grid-cell-wise regression, CNNs, or LSTMs, we adapt a multi-variate time series graph neural network (MTGNN) architecture, which was originally developed for mobility and traffic forecasting on urban sensor networks to this satellite-geodesy task. Spatial dependencies are encoded in a static, interpretable hybrid adjacency matrix that combines geodesic proximity with lagged correlations of climatic time series, capturing both local hydrological coupling and large-scale teleconnections. The reconstruction achieves a grid-cell Pearson correlation of 0.69, a basin-mean correlation of 0.94, and a near-zero bias, and it reproduces the spatial fingerprints of the 2015/16 El Niño and 2020/21 La Niña events. A systematic comparison with established reconstruction approaches (GTWS-MLrec, RM-REC, GRAiCE) shows that the graph-based model is statistically competitive at basin scale, reaching a correlation within 0.025 of the best baseline while using only roughly half to a tenth of the predictors the other models require and revealing characteristic weaknesses in arid regions in all models. The complete implementation is publicly available at github.com/hcu-cml/MTGNN-TWS-Reconstruction-GRACE

中文摘要翻译: 陆地水储量（TWS）整合了积雪、土壤水分、地表水和地下水，是反映气候变率和人类活动重塑全球水循环的关键指标。GRACE和GRACE-FO卫星任务提供了唯一直接、全球一致的TWS变化观测，但其记录始于2002年，对于许多气候尺度分析而言时间跨度过短。本文提出一种深度学习应用，通过学习每日ERA5气象强迫（降水、蒸散发、径流）与月度GRACE观测之间的关系，将月度GRACE类TWS异常（TWSA）重建回溯至1940年。与先前基于网格单元回归、CNN或LSTM的重建方法不同，我们将最初为城市传感器网络上的移动性和交通预测而开发的多变量时间序列图神经网络（MTGNN）架构适配于这一卫星大地测量任务。空间依赖关系通过一个静态、可解释的混合邻接矩阵进行编码，该矩阵结合了测地邻近性与气候时间序列的滞后相关性，同时捕捉局地水文耦合和大尺度遥相关。该重建实现了网格单元皮尔逊相关系数0.69、流域均值相关系数0.94，且偏差接近零，并成功再现了2015/16年厄尔尼诺和2020/21年拉尼娜事件的空间特征。与已有重建方法（GTWS-MLrec、RM-REC、GRAiCE）的系统比较表明，基于图模型的方法在流域尺度上具有统计竞争力，在使用仅约其他模型一半至十分之一预测因子的情况下，达到与最优基线相差0.025以内的相关系数，并揭示了所有模型在干旱区域的共同特征性弱点。完整实现已开源于github.com/hcu-cml/MTGNN-TWS-Reconstruction-GRACE。

</details>


### [19] [The Degeneracy Distillery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23838)
*T. Lucas Makinen,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Niall Jeffrey,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"退化蒸馏器"方法，通过估计和展平Fisher信息矩阵，自动且符号化地检测和解决物理模型中的参数退化问题，能发现使参数对数据产生独立影响的符号坐标变换，将下游神经后验估计所需的模拟预算降低最多10倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 参数或标签产生相似数据时会出现退化现象，这使标签预测和逆问题求解变得困难。现有方法大多基于后验分布仅在单点处展平，缺乏全局性。因此需要一种自动、符号化的方法来检测和解决物理模型中的退化参数组合。

Method: 提出退化蒸馏器方法，仅从参数-数据（或参数-仿真）对出发，通过估计和展平Fisher信息矩阵，自动且符号化地检测和解决退化参数组合。该方法探索似然的信息几何，将退化视为物理模型的内在属性，无需真实数据观测，并通过符号坐标变换识别使参数对数据产生独立影响的组合。

Result: 在合成和真实世界问题上展示了该方法的效果：发现的符号坐标变换能全局展平Fisher信息的期望（而非后验方法仅在单点处展平）；在匹配验证校准条件下，将神经后验估计所需的模拟预算减少最多10倍，同时获得对系统的物理洞察。

Conclusion: 该方法能自动识别物理模型中的参数退化结构，通过符号坐标变换实现全局信息几何展平，显著提升下游推断效率并保持物理可解释性。

Original Abstract: When two or more parameters or labels produce similar data, they are degenerate, or hard to distinguish. Degeneracies render both label prediction and inverse problems difficult, since both machine learning algorithms and probabilistic samplers rely on the distinguishability of data and its gradients with respect to parameters. However, identifying degeneracies in physical models or real-world datasets can be elucidating about the choice of model or the underlying process that produces the data. We present the degeneracy distillery, a method that (1) detects and (2) resolves degenerate parameter combinations (a) automatically and (b) symbolically, from parameter-data (or parameter-simulation) pairs alone, through estimation and flattening of the Fisher information matrix. By exploring the information geometry of the likelihood, we characterize degeneracies as an intrinsic property of the physical model, requiring no realised data observation. We demonstrate our approach on a range of synthetic and real-world problems, discovering symbolic coordinate transformations that identify the combinations of parameters of a model which yield independent effects on the data. The resulting coordinates flatten the Fisher information in expectation globally, in contrast to posterior-based methods that flatten only at a single point, and substantially reduce the simulation budget required for downstream neural posterior estimation. In test cases we require up to $10\times$ fewer simulations for posterior estimation at matched validation calibration whilst simultaneously gaining physical insight on the system.

中文摘要翻译: 当两个或多个参数或标签产生相似数据时，它们就是退化的，或者难以区分。退化性使得标签预测和逆问题都变得困难，因为机器学习算法和概率采样器都依赖于数据的可区分性及其相对于参数的梯度。然而，识别物理模型或真实数据集中的退化现象，有助于深入理解模型的选择或产生数据的底层过程。我们提出了"退化蒸馏器"方法，该方法能够仅从参数-数据（或参数-仿真）对出发，（1）自动且（2）符号化地检测和解决退化参数组合，通过估计和展平Fisher信息矩阵实现。通过探索似然的信息几何，我们将退化表征为物理模型的内在属性，无需真实数据观测。我们在多种合成和真实世界问题上展示了该方法，发现了能够识别模型中产生独立数据效应的参数组合的符号坐标变换。得到的坐标能够全局展平Fisher信息的期望，而基于后验的方法仅在单点处展平，并且显著减少了下游神经后验估计所需的模拟预算。在测试案例中，我们在匹配验证校准的条件下，将后验估计所需的模拟次数减少了最多10倍，同时获得了对系统的物理洞察。

</details>


### [20] [Machine Learning Modeling for Real-Time Melt Pool Monitoring in Laser Powder Bed Fusion Additive Manufacturing: A Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23851)
*Inioluwa Emmanuel,Zhuo Yang,Ho Yeung,Xinyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合预训练CNN特征（EfficientNetB0）与随机森林的混合方法，用于激光粉末床熔融增材制造中的熔池图像实时异常检测，在平衡小数据集（1200张）上实现了0.9458准确率和亚毫秒级推理速度（1.15ms/张），优于纯深度学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在数据有限的增材制造环境中，现有纯深度学习方法推理时间长、计算开销大，难以满足工厂车间实时监控的部署需求，亟需一种兼具高精度和高计算效率的熔池异常检测方案。

Method: 构建了包含1200张镍基高温合金625熔池图像的平衡二分类数据集；对比了五种方法：三种迁移学习架构（ResNet50、EfficientNetB0、MobileNetV2）和两种随机森林方法（基于EfficientNetB0特征嵌入的混合方法、基于原始像素特征的基线方法）；采用80/20训练-测试划分及90/10验证划分，并进行标准化预处理和数据增强。

Result: 混合EfficientNetB0+随机森林方法在测试集上取得最佳性能：F1分数0.9451，准确率0.9458，AUC 0.9904，推理时间仅1.15ms/张；纯深度学习方法推理时间显著更高且准确率较低。

Conclusion: 将预训练卷积特征与经典集成学习方法相结合，为数据有限的增材制造环境中的实时熔池异常检测提供了一条鲁棒且计算高效的途径。

Original Abstract: This work investigates the implementation of artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML) for real-time monitoring in laser powder bed fusion (LPBF) additive manufacturing. We developed a binary image classification framework for distinguishing normal and abnormal melt pool images using a balanced dataset of 1,200 images collected from Nickel superalloy 625 on the NIST AMMT platform. The study evaluates accuracy and inference time based on control requirements and hardware limitations of open-architecture LPBF machines. We benchmark three transfer learning architectures (ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and MobileNetV2) against two Random Forest approaches: one trained on EfficientNetB0 feature embeddings (hybrid) and one trained on raw pixel features (baseline). Images are stratified into 80/20 train-test splits, with a further 90/10 validation split on the training set, and undergo standardized resizing, normalization, and label-preserving data augmentation to emulate realistic process variability. Each model is evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, and area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), along with training time, inference latency, and CPU & GPU usage to capture deployability constraints relevant to factory-floor monitoring. The hybrid EfficientNetB0-plus-Random Forest approach achieves the best performance on the held-out test set, with an F1 score of 0.9451, accuracy of 0.9458, and AUC of 0.9904, while maintaining sub-millisecond per-image inference (1.15 ms). In contrast, purely deep learning models exhibit significantly higher inference times with lower accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining pre-trained convolutional features with classical ensemble methods provides a robust, computationally efficient route to real-time melt pool anomaly detection in data-limited additive manufacturing environments.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究探讨了人工智能与机器学习在激光粉末床熔融增材制造中用于实时监控的实现方案。我们开发了一个二值图像分类框架，用于区分正常和异常的熔池图像，该框架基于从NIST AMMT平台上采集的镍基高温合金625的1,200张图像构成的平衡数据集。研究基于开放式架构LPBF设备的控制需求和硬件限制，评估了准确率和推理时间。我们对三种迁移学习架构（ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和MobileNetV2）与两种随机森林方法进行了基准比较：一种基于EfficientNetB0特征嵌入（混合方法），另一种基于原始像素特征（基线方法）。图像按80/20比例分层划分为训练集和测试集，训练集进一步按90/10比例划分验证集，并经过标准化的尺寸调整、归一化以及保持标签的数据增强，以模拟真实工艺变异性。每个模型均使用准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数和受试者工作特征曲线下面积进行评估，同时记录训练时间、推理延迟及CPU与GPU使用率，以捕捉与工厂车间监控部署约束相关的指标。混合EfficientNetB0加随机森林方法在留出测试集上取得了最佳性能：F1分数为0.9451，准确率为0.9458，AUC为0.9904，同时保持每张图像亚毫秒级的推理速度（1.15 ms）。相比之下，纯深度学习模型的推理时间显著更高且准确率更低。这些结果表明，将预训练卷积特征与经典集成方法相结合，为数据有限的增材制造环境中的实时熔池异常检测提供了一条鲁棒且计算高效的途径。

</details>


### [21] [Sesame: Structure-Aware Molecular Generation via Spatial Density-Map Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23856)
*Konstantin Yatsenko,Arvind Thiagarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sesame是一种基于扩散模型的分子生成框架，通过空间对偶变换器模块同时支持从头药物分子设计和基于片段的先导化合物优化，并在配体-蛋白质联合数据集上训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型往往只关注从头分子生成或蛋白质-配体相互作用建模，缺乏对先导化合物优化中关键操作——从部分起始结构（片段或骨架）生长分子的支持，且缺乏统一的机制来同时处理从头生成和片段条件化生成。

Method: 提出Sesame模型，核心是空间对偶变换器模块，将部分分子结构和周围蛋白口袋均表示为连续空间密度图并作为条件；采用扩散框架联合去噪原子类型、键类型和位置；引入轨迹微调方案，在模型自身的采样轨迹上训练以提升生成质量。

Result: 在配体-only和蛋白质-配体大型数据集上训练，实现了从头生成和片段条件化的先导化合物优化两种功能。

Conclusion: Sesame通过统一的条件机制同时支持从头分子设计和先导化合物优化，为计算药物设计提供了实用的生成框架。

Original Abstract: Generative molecular models for drug design are a promising direction with much active research. In the next phase of computational drug design, such models will need to understand small molecule structure and protein-ligand interactions, and they will need to possess the machinery to generate molecules de novo. Incorporating each feature poses a critical challenge. Equally important, yet often treated as secondary, is the ability to grow a molecule from a partial starting point -- a scaffold or fragment supplied by a chemist -- which is the central operation of lead optimization. We present Sesame (Spatial Evoformer for a Structure-Aware Molecular Engine), a diffusion-based molecular generation model that leverages a novel spatial pairformer module to condition on partial molecular structure and the surrounding protein pocket, both expressed as continuous spatial density maps. This single conditioning mechanism supports both de novo generation and fragment-conditioned lead optimization, letting a medicinal chemist prune a hit to a scaffold and have Sesame grow it in productive ways. In addition to this module, we also introduce a diffusion framework for joint denoising of atom types, bond types, and positions, along with a trajectory finetuning scheme that trains on the model's own sampling rollouts to improve generation quality. Sesame is trained on a large corpus of ligand-only and protein-ligand datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 用于药物设计的生成分子模型是一个充满前景且活跃的研究方向。在计算药物设计的下一阶段，这类模型需要理解小分子结构和蛋白质-配体相互作用，并具备从头生成分子的能力。融入每一类特征都构成了关键挑战。同样重要但常被视为次要的，是从部分起始点（化学家提供的骨架或片段）生长分子的能力——这是先导化合物优化的核心操作。我们提出了Sesame（结构感知分子的空间演化变换器），一个基于扩散的分子生成模型，它利用新颖的空间对偶变换器模块，以部分分子结构和周围蛋白口袋为条件，两者均表示为连续空间密度图。这种单一的条件机制同时支持从头生成和片段条件化的先导化合物优化，使药物化学家能够将先导化合物修剪为骨架，并让Sesame以有效的方式将其生长。除了该模块外，我们还引入了一个用于原子类型、键类型和位置联合去噪的扩散框架，以及一个轨迹微调方案，该方案在模型自身的采样轨迹上训练以提升生成质量。Sesame在配体-only和蛋白质-配体大型数据集上进行了训练。

</details>


### [22] [Are Safety Guarantees in Neural Networks Safe? How to Compute Trustworthy Robustness Certifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23858)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边心距（apothem）度量的鲁棒性认证方法，通过线性次Oracle调用即可计算最优认证区间，并证明体积最优的Oracle算法在理论上不可行。同时引入对偶认证概念和ParallelepipedoNN系统，在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上实现至少两倍的最小边长改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的鲁棒性认证方法大多追求最大化认证区域的体积，但近期研究表明计算体积最优的认证在合理时间内不可行（难解性结果）。因此需要一种计算高效且理论上可保证最优性的替代度量与方法。

Method: 1) 引入边心距（apothem）度量来衡量认证区域的质量；2) 证明边心距最优的认证可在对NN验证器（Oracle）进行线性次调用（相对于输入域直径）时计算得到；3) 从理论上证明不存在体积最优的基于Oracle的算法；4) 引入对偶认证（dual certification）概念，提供一类样本的区间上界；5) 构建ParallelepipedoNN系统实现上述方法。

Result: 在标准MNIST和Fashion MNIST基准测试上的实验表明，与现有工作在相同数据集上的初步比较显示，在最小边长指标上至少有2倍的改进。

Conclusion: 边心距度量是鲁棒性认证中一个高效且理论上可保证最优性的替代方案；对偶认证可为模型鲁棒性提供更紧的上界；ParallelepipedoNN系统在标准数据集上取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。

Original Abstract: A primary challenge in AI safety is the existence of adversarial examples -- slightly distorted inputs that cause a neural network (NN) to misclassify. To mitigate this problem, recent research focuses on the computation of robustness certifications, which, for a given input, determine the largest distortion the input may receive without breaking the network's prediction. Robustness certifications can be interpreted as an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle (multi-dimensional intervals). Most existing approaches focus on maximizing the certification's volume, but recent intractability results prohibit the computation of volume-optimal certifications in reasonable time. We introduce the apothem measure and show how to compute apothem-optimal certifications in a linear number of calls to a NN verifier (oracle) w.r.t. the input domain's diameter. Moreover, we prove that we cannot have a volume-optimal, oracle-based algorithm, even if we discard the oracle costs. Also, we introduce dual certifications -- an interval including all instances of a class -- thus providing apothem-minimum upper bounds to a robustness certification. Further, we present the ParallelepipedoNN system, which we evaluate on the standard MNIST and Fashion MNIST benchmarks. A preliminary comparison with existing work on the same datasets reveals at least two-fold improvement w.r.t. the minimum edge length.

中文摘要翻译: 人工智能安全的一个主要挑战是 adversarial examples 的存在——即微小的输入扰动会导致神经网络（NN）误分类。为了缓解这一问题，近期研究聚焦于鲁棒性认证的计算，该认证对于一个给定输入，确定该输入在不破坏网络预测的前提下可以承受的最大扰动。鲁棒性认证可以被解释为轴对齐的超矩形（多维区间）。大多数现有方法侧重于最大化认证的体积，但近期的难解性结果使得在合理时间内计算体积最优的认证变得不可行。我们引入了边心距度量，并展示了如何在对NN验证器（Oracle）进行相对于输入域直径的线性次调用时，计算边心距最优的认证。此外，我们证明了即使忽略Oracle的代价，也不存在一种体积最优的、基于Oracle的算法。同时，我们引入了对偶认证——一个包含某个类别所有实例的区间——从而为鲁棒性认证提供边心距最小的上界。进一步，我们提出了ParallelepipedoNN系统，并在标准MNIST和Fashion MNIST基准上对其进行了评估。与现有工作在相同数据集上的初步比较显示，在最小边长指标上至少有两倍的改进。

</details>


### [23] [Exact Schur-Sylvester Dimensionality Reductions for Non-Smooth Stochastic Complexity and Manifold Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23867)
*Trenton Lau,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确且计算高效的NML编码长度计算方法，通过利用块Schur补和Sylvester行列式恒等式，将投影算子和体积因子的计算复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³+N²k)，在高维数据集上实现了超过14,100倍的加速，同时保持双精度数值等价。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非光滑估计器（如Lasso）的精确NML编码长度计算长期以来受限于流形约束投影和体积积分带来的三次方计算瓶颈，导致在大规模统计推断中难以应用。

Method: 利用块Schur补和Sylvester行列式恒等式，重新表述PPMH采样器中投影算子和体积因子的计算，避免了对大规模KKT矩阵的求逆和Gram矩阵行列式的直接计算，将复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³+N²k)。

Result: 理论上证明了计算复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³+N²k)；实验上在高维数据集上实现了超过14,100倍的恒定加速，同时保持双精度数值等价性。

Conclusion: 该工作使得精确的非光滑NML估计在大规模统计推断中变得高度可行，为稀疏支持向量机、弹性网络和组Lasso等模型提供了高效计算方案。

Original Abstract: The exact computation of the Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) codelength for regular non-smooth estimators (e.g., Lasso) has been historically limited by the cubic scaling walls of manifold-constrained projection and volume integration. At each step of the geometric Propose-and-Project Metropolis--Hastings (PPMH) sampler, evaluating the projection operator requires inverting an $(N+k) \times (N+k)$ generalized KKT matrix, while calculating the volume factor requires the determinant of an $(N-k) \times (N-k)$ Gram matrix. This paper presents an exact, mathematically equivalent formulation that bypasses both bottlenecks by utilizing the block Schur complement and Sylvester's determinant identity. We prove that the computational complexity of both operations collapses from $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ to $\mathcal{O}(k^3 + N^2 k)$ per step. We generalize this reduction to Sparse Support Vector Machines (SVMs), Elastic Net, and Group Lasso. Finally, we provide a rigorous numerical stability analysis and evaluate the sampler's efficiency using the Effective Sample Size (ESS) per second. Our empirical benchmarks on high-dimensional datasets confirm a constant speedup exceeding $14{,}100\times$ while maintaining double-precision numerical equivalence, rendering exact non-smooth NML estimation highly tractable for large-scale statistical inference.

中文摘要翻译: 对于正则非光滑估计器（如Lasso），精确计算归一化最大似然（NML）编码长度历来受限于流形约束投影和体积积分带来的三次方计算瓶颈。在几何Propose-and-Project Metropolis–Hastings（PPMH）采样器的每一步中，计算投影算子需要对一个(N+k)×(N+k)的广义KKT矩阵求逆，而计算体积因子则需要一个(N−k)×(N−k)的Gram矩阵的行列式。本文提出了一种精确且数学等价的重新表述，通过利用块Schur补和Sylvester行列式恒等式绕过了这两个瓶颈。我们证明，两种操作的计算复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³+N²k)每步。我们将这一加速推广到稀疏支持向量机（SVM）、弹性网络和组Lasso。最后，我们提供了严格的数值稳定性分析，并使用每秒有效样本量（ESS）评估了采样器的效率。我们在高维数据集上的实证基准测试证实，在保持双精度数值等价的前提下，实现了超过14,100倍的恒定加速，使得精确的非光滑NML估计在大规模统计推断中变得高度可行。

</details>


### [24] [Federated Survival Analysis in Healthcare: A Multi-Model Evaluation on Cross-Institutional Heterogeneous Breast Cancer Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23871)
*Natalia Moreno-Blasco,Anusha Ihalapathirana,Pekka Siirtola,Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在真实跨机构乳腺癌队列中系统评估了联邦学习在生存分析中的有效性，发现联邦学习一致优于本地训练，接近甚至偶尔超过集中式性能，其中随机生存森林（RSF）在异质性客户间表现最佳，FedAvg和FedProx比FedAdam更稳定，并据此给出了面向决策的实践指南。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可靠的生存时间预测模型需要大规模多样化队列，但单一机构难以获取，而隐私法规禁止患者数据集中化。联邦学习虽提供隐私保护替代方案，但其在现实异质性条件下的生存建模效果尚不明确，亟需系统评估。

Method: 在具有自然异质性分布式客户的跨机构乳腺癌队列上，系统比较了三种代表性生存模型（Cox比例风险模型、DeepSurv、随机生存森林RSF）在集中式、本地和联邦训练下的表现，并对基于梯度的模型评估了三种联邦优化策略（FedAvg、FedProx、FedAdam）。

Result: 联邦学习一致优于本地训练，接近甚至偶尔超过集中式性能；RSF在异质性客户间实现了判别力、校准度和鲁棒性的最佳平衡；性能依赖于客户分布的多样性；FedAvg和FedProx比FedAdam更强大且更稳定。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，推导出面向决策的实践指南，将数据、隐私、可解释性和资源约束映射到推荐的模型和训练范式选择，为医疗场景中的联邦生存建模提供参考。

Original Abstract: Survival analysis is central to clinical decision-making, yet reliable time-to-event models require large, diverse cohorts that are rarely available at a single institution, while privacy regulations restrict the centralization of patient data. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative by training shared models without exchanging raw data, but its effectiveness for survival modeling under realistic, heterogeneous conditions remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a systematic, multi-model evaluation of federated survival analysis on a cross-institutional breast cancer cohort with naturally heterogeneous distributed clients. Three representative survival models, the Cox Proportional Hazards model, DeepSurv, and Random Survival Forest (RSF), are compared across centralized, local, and federated training, and three federated optimization strategies (FedAvg, FedProx, and FedAdam) are assessed for the gradient-based models. Results show that FL consistently outperforms local training and approaches, and occasionally exceeds, centralized performance, while RSF offers the best overall balance of discrimination, calibration, and robustness across heterogeneous clients. We further find that performance depends on the diversity of client distributions, and that FedAvg and FedProx are stronger and more stable than FedAdam. Based on these findings, we derive practical, decision-oriented guidelines mapping data, privacy, interpretability, and resource constraints to recommended model and training-paradigm choices for federated survival modeling in healthcare.

中文摘要翻译: 生存分析是临床决策的核心，但可靠的时间-事件模型需要大规模、多样化的队列，而单一机构难以获取这样的数据，同时隐私法规限制了对患者数据的集中化处理。联邦学习（FL）通过在不交换原始数据的情况下训练共享模型，提供了一种保护隐私的替代方案，但其在现实异质性条件下进行生存建模的有效性仍未被充分理解。本文在一个具有自然异质性分布式客户的跨机构乳腺癌队列上，对联邦生存分析进行了系统的多模型评估。研究比较了三种代表性生存模型——Cox比例风险模型、DeepSurv和随机生存森林（RSF）——在集中式、本地和联邦训练下的表现，并针对基于梯度的模型评估了三种联邦优化策略（FedAvg、FedProx和FedAdam）。结果表明，联邦学习一致优于本地训练，接近甚至偶尔超过集中式性能，而RSF在异质性客户间提供了判别力、校准度和鲁棒性的最佳整体平衡。我们进一步发现，性能取决于客户分布的多样性，并且FedAvg和FedProx比FedAdam更强大、更稳定。基于这些发现，我们推导出面向决策的实践指南，将数据、隐私、可解释性和资源约束映射到医疗保健中联邦生存建模的推荐模型和训练范式选择。

</details>


### [25] [MGI: Member vs Generated Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23872)
*Bihe Zhao,Michel Meintz,Juangui Xu,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出成员vs生成推理（MGI）问题，指出现有成员推断和归因方法在区分训练成员和模型生成样本时的系统性缺陷，并提出数据断路器（DCB）方法，利用生成模型的自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号来解决该问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着生成模型生成与人类创作难以区分的内容，以及模型可能记忆并复现训练数据，判断一个样本是训练集成员还是模型自身生成变得困难。现有成员推断方法系统性地将生成样本误判为训练成员，而归因方法则常将真实成员误判为生成样本，原因在于两者都依赖似然相关信号，而该信号对训练样本和模型输出同样偏高。

Method: 提出Data Circuit Breaker (DCB)方法，采用三阶段流程，结合生成模型的自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号，来区分训练成员与生成样本。

Result: 在包括图像自回归模型和扩散模型等多种生成模型上，DCB一致地解决了成员推断和归因方法的缺陷；即使在模型生成训练样本的近似重复时仍保持有效；并能泛化到基于生成数据训练新模型的挑战性衍生设置中。

Conclusion: DCB方法能够有效区分训练成员与生成样本，克服了现有方法在MGI任务上的系统性失败。

Original Abstract: As generative models increasingly produce samples that are indistinguishable from human-created content, it becomes difficult to determine whether a given data point was part of a model's natural training set or was generated by the model itself, especially when models memorize and reproduce training data. We formalize this challenge as Member vs Generated Inference (MGI): given a sample and a target generative model, infer whether the sample is a true training member or a generated output of that model. Focusing on image generation, we show that existing membership inference methods systematically misclassify generated samples as training members, while attribution-based methods often misclassify true members as generated. This failure arises because both approaches rely on likelihood-related signals that are similarly elevated for training examples and for the model's own outputs. To address MGI, we propose Data Circuit Breaker (DCB), a three-stage method that combines complementary signals from a generative model's autoencoder and latent generator to distinguish training members from generated samples. Across multiple generative models, including image autoregressive and diffusion models, DCB consistently addresses the shortcomings of membership inference and attribution methods, remains effective even when models reproduce near-duplicates of training samples, and generalizes to challenging model derivative settings in which new models are trained on generated data.

中文摘要翻译: 随着生成模型越来越多地产生与人类创作内容难以区分的样本，判断一个给定的数据点是模型自然训练集的一部分还是由模型本身生成变得困难，尤其是当模型记忆并复现训练数据时。我们将这一挑战形式化为成员vs生成推理（Member vs Generated Inference, MGI）：给定一个样本和一个目标生成模型，推断该样本是真实的训练成员还是该模型的生成输出。聚焦于图像生成，我们发现现有的成员推断方法系统性地将生成样本误分类为训练成员，而基于归因的方法则常常将真实成员误分类为生成样本。这一失败源于两种方法都依赖于似然相关信号，而该信号对于训练样例和模型自身输出同样偏高。为了解决MGI问题，我们提出了数据断路器（Data Circuit Breaker, DCB），一种三阶段方法，通过结合生成模型的自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号来区分训练成员与生成样本。在包括图像自回归模型和扩散模型在内的多种生成模型上，DCB一致地解决了成员推断和归因方法的缺陷，即使当模型生成训练样本的近似重复时仍保持有效，并且能够泛化到具有挑战性的模型衍生设置中，即新模型在生成数据上训练的情况。

</details>


### [26] [GRACE: Gated Refinement for Accurate Causal Edge Discovery in High-Dimensional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23880)
*Mohammad Fesanghary,Abhinav Havaldar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRACE 提出了一种结合约束性方法和门控机制的因果发现框架，利用 Hard Concrete 门和 L0 正则化实现边缘的鲁棒二元决策，在不使用昂贵非线性条件独立性检验的情况下，以线性复杂度实现高精度因果图学习，在合成数据和真实河流量数据集上均取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代时间序列数据维度高、变量交互复杂，传统的因果发现方法面临两难：基于约束的方法的非线性条件独立性检验计算成本过高，而基于评分的方法虽避免检验但需要人为设定阈值将连续边缘分数二值化，导致决策边界不清晰。

Method: GRACE 首先通过快速线性条件独立性检验获取高召回率的候选骨架，然后为每条候选边引入独立的 Hard Concrete 门控机制，结合 L0 正则化训练一个门控模型；门的输出值自然地集中于 0 或 1 附近，形成清晰的双峰分布，从而无需人工阈值即可完成边缘的鲁棒二值决策；正则化强度根据问题维度和骨架密度自动调整。

Result: 在多种图拓扑（无标度、Erdős-R'enyi、小世界）和最高 100 维的合成基准上，GRACE 相比基础条件独立性方法 F1 显著提升，同时保持高精确率；优于注意力机制和基于评分的方法；在几乎同等效果下比昂贵的非线性条件独立性检验快 75 倍；在易北河真实流量数据上，时态自举变体从 106 个假阳性中恢复出 11 条因果边中的 9 条，仅 1 个误报（F1=0.86, AUROC=0.99），假阳性减少 99%。

Conclusion: GRACE 通过门控机制与 L0 正则化的结合，在保持高召回率的同时有效消除了基于约束方法的假阳性，且计算效率远超非线性检验方法，为高维时间序列因果发现提供了一种兼具精确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性的实用方案。

Original Abstract: From climate teleconnections to gene regulation, modern time-series datasets encompass tens or hundreds of interacting variables, making causal discovery increasingly challenging. Constraint-based methods offer statistical rigor but their nonlinear CI tests are infeasible at scale, while score-based alternatives avoid CI testing but require arbitrary thresholds to binarize continuous edge scores. We propose GRACE ($\textbf{G}$ated $\textbf{R}$efinement for $\textbf{A}$ccurate $\textbf{C}$ausal $\textbf{E}$dge discovery), which refines constraint-based discovery using Hard Concrete gates with $L_0$ regularization: each candidate edge has an independent gate whose values concentrate near 0 or 1, yielding a clean bimodal separation that makes the binary decision robust, unlike the narrow, overlapping score distributions produced by $L_1$ and attention-based methods. A fast linear CI skeleton provides high-recall candidates; a single gated model then prunes false positives by learning which edges genuinely improve prediction, with automatic regularization adapted to problem dimensions and skeleton density. Systematic experiments on synthetic benchmarks, spanning diverse graph topologies (scale-free, Erdős-R'enyi, small-world) and dimensionalities up to $d=100$, show that GRACE substantially improves F1 over its base CI method while maintaining high precision, and outperforms attention-based and score-based alternatives. GRACE matches or exceeds expensive nonlinear CI tests at a fraction of the cost ($75\times$ faster). On a real-world river flow dataset, where rainfall confounders, variable propagation lags, and distributional shifts violate standard assumptions, a temporal bootstrap variant of GRACE recovers 9 of 11 causal edges along the Elbe River with only 1 false positive ($F_1 = 0.86$, AUROC${} = 0.99$), reducing the skeleton's 106 false positives by 99%.

中文摘要翻译: 从气候遥相关到基因调控，现代时间序列数据集包含数十或数百个交互变量，使得因果发现日益具有挑战性。基于约束的方法具有统计严谨性，但其非线性条件独立性检验在大规模场景下不可行；而基于评分的方法虽避免了条件独立性检验，却需要人为设定阈值来将连续的边缘分数二值化。我们提出 GRACE（门控精化实现准确因果边缘发现），该方法利用带有 L0 正则化的 Hard Concrete 门来精化基于约束的因果发现：每条候选边具有一个独立的门，其值集中于 0 或 1 附近，产生清晰的双峰分离，使得二元决策具有鲁棒性，这与 L1 正则化和注意力方法产生的狭窄且重叠的分数分布形成鲜明对比。一个快速的线性条件独立性检验骨架提供高召回率的候选边；随后一个单一的门控模型通过学习哪些边真正能提升预测性能来剪除假阳性，其正则化强度根据问题维度和骨架密度自动调整。在涵盖多种图拓扑（无标度、Erdős-R'enyi、小世界）和高达 100 维的系统性合成基准实验表明，GRACE 在保持高精确率的同时，显著提升了相对于其基础条件独立性方法的 F1 分数，并且优于基于注意力和基于评分的方法。GRACE 以极低的计算成本匹配甚至超越昂贵的非线性条件独立性检验（快 75 倍）。在一个真实世界的河流流量数据集上，该数据集存在降雨混杂因子、变量传播滞后和分布偏移等违反标准假设的情况，GRACE 的时态自举变体在易北河上恢复了 11 条因果边中的 9 条，仅出现 1 个假阳性（F1=0.86，AUROC=0.99），将骨架的 106 个假阳性减少了 99%。

</details>


### [27] [ARIA: Adaptive Region-Based Importance Allocation for Conditional Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23898)
*Loay Mualem,Vinh Tong,Samir Darouich,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ARIA框架，通过自适应分配训练资源到条件空间中差异较大的区域，提升条件扩散模型知识蒸馏的效果，尤其在未见过和欠表示区域表现显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 条件扩散模型的知识蒸馏面临一个重要瓶颈：由于预测噪声强烈依赖于条件信号，有效蒸馏需要探索庞大的条件空间，而配对图像-条件数据有限、生成合成图像计算成本高昂，现有方法通过切换条件来扩展空间，但未解决训练资源如何在不同条件间合理分配的问题。

Method: 提出ARIA框架，在条件空间的粗粒度区域上自适应分配训练努力。通过在线估计教师-学生在区域级别的差异，将更新集中在仍存在不对齐的区域，同时保留原始蒸馏目标。理论分析证明了在有限方差和漂移假设下，所提追踪机制能跟随训练过程中不断演化的差异。

Result: 实验表明，ARIA在大多数架构和设置下优于RC（Random Condition）方法，在未见过和欠表示的条件区域中增益最为明显。同时提供了理论分析，证明追踪机制在有限方差和漂移假设下有效。

Conclusion: 自适应分配训练资源是提升条件扩散模型蒸馏效果的有效策略，尤其能显著改善在分布外和欠表示条件区域的蒸馏性能。

Original Abstract: Distilling conditional diffusion models aims to transfer the behavior of a large teacher to a smaller student while preserving alignment across conditioning inputs. Unlike recognition tasks, knowledge distillation in conditional diffusion often struggles to transfer knowledge beyond the training distribution, since the predicted noise strongly depends on the conditioning signal. As a result, effective distillation requires exploring a large conditioning space. In practical settings, this creates a major bottleneck. Paired image-condition data may be limited, and generating synthetic images for every available condition is often computationally infeasible, while the pool of conditions, such as text prompts, can be extremely large. Recent work addresses this issue by switching conditions during training, exposing the student to a broader conditioning space without changing the distillation objective. Yet this raises a complementary question: once a large conditioning corpus is available, how should the training effort be allocated? In this work, we introduce ARIA, a framework that adaptively allocates training effort across coarse regions of the conditioning space. By maintaining online estimates of teacher-student discrepancy at the region level, ARIA focuses updates where misalignment persists while preserving the original distillation objective. Empirically, ARIA improves over RC across most architectures and settings, with the clearest gains observed in unseen and underrepresented regimes. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that the proposed tracking mechanism follows the evolving discrepancy during training under bounded variance and drift assumptions.

中文摘要翻译: 条件扩散模型的蒸馏旨在将大型教师模型的行为迁移到较小的学生模型上，同时保持对不同条件输入的对齐。与识别任务不同，条件扩散模型中的知识蒸馏往往难以将知识迁移到训练分布之外，因为预测噪声强烈依赖于条件信号。因此，有效的蒸馏需要探索庞大的条件空间。在实际场景中，这造成了主要瓶颈：配对的图像-条件数据可能有限，为每个可用条件生成合成图像通常计算上不可行，而条件池（如文本提示）可能极其庞大。近期工作通过训练过程中切换条件来缓解此问题，使学生暴露于更广泛的条件空间而不改变蒸馏目标。但这引出了一个补充性问题：一旦拥有大规模条件语料库，应如何分配训练资源？本文提出ARIA框架，该框架在条件空间的粗粒度区域上自适应分配训练努力。通过维护区域级别的教师-学生差异的在线估计，ARIA将更新集中在不对齐仍然存在的区域，同时保留原始蒸馏目标。实验表明，ARIA在大多数架构和设置下优于RC方法，在未见过和欠表示的条件区域中增益最为明显。我们还提供了理论分析，表明在有限方差和漂移假设下，所提追踪机制能跟随训练过程中不断演化的差异。

</details>


### [28] [Closing the Loop: Formally Verified Law as a Reward Signal for Self-Improving Legal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23913)
*Armin Heydari,Torben Leowald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于法律AI训练的形式化可验证奖励信号架构，借鉴数学AI中“LLM提议，验证器处置”的范式，通过自动形式化、验证内核和解释生成三部分，在法律的计算性组件上提供可证明的正确性，在开放纹理法律分析上提供结构性保证。在德国法程序期限计算、美国宪法商业条款分析和跨境制裁比例性上得到验证，并展示了其在法律AI训练中的结构性优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 法律AI训练面临的核心挑战是缺乏可验证的奖励信号，传统的强化学习回路在法律领域难以闭环。现有方法无法同时保证法律推理的计算正确性和论证结构性完整性。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的自动形式化架构，将法律文本转换为扩展Catala的形式化法律演算，结合验证内核确保推理正确性，并通过形式化证明轨迹生成解释。采用“LLM提议，验证器处置”范式，由外部确定性验证器提供可验证结果来封闭强化学习回路。

Result: 在德国法程序期限计算、美国宪法商业条款分析和跨境制裁比例性三个不同法律领域验证了架构有效性。证明了该架构在法律AI训练中具有结构性优势：确定性外部验证器可为法律问题提供可验证结果，从而弥补传统强化学习回路在法律领域的缺口。

Conclusion: 该架构成功地将形式化验证方法引入法律AI领域，不仅提供了计算组件的可证明正确性，还为开放纹理法律分析提供了结构性保证。更重要的是，该架构天然适用于法律AI训练，通过可验证的奖励信号解决了法律领域强化学习的关键瓶颈。
